## MIRAE ASSET SECURITIES 2022 INTEGRATED REPORT

We are committed to pioneering a sustainable future through investment and asset management

<!-- image -->

## About This Report

The 2022 Mirae Asset Securities Integrated Report was prepared with the aim of reviewing our sustainability business activities and achievements, thus providing stakeholders with information in a transparent manner.

## Reporting Status

This is the 11th sustainability report since we published it for the first time in the industry in 2006. It has been developed as an Integrated Report which consolidates the annual report and a sustainability report every year since 2018.

## Reporting Principles

This report is compiled in accordance with the Core option of the Global Reporting Initiative (GRI) Standards, a global standard for sustainability reports, the framework of the International Integrated Reporting Council (IIRC), and the Sustainability Accounting Standards Board (SASB) Standards. It is also aligned with the Ten Principles of the UN Global Compact.

## Reporting Period

This report covers sustainability business activities and performances for the year from January 1 to December 31, 2021, including some activities from the first half of 2022. Some quantitative topics disclose three-year data from 2019 for trend comparison, and information that does not fit such criteria will include footnotes.

## Reporting Scope

The scope of information in this report encompasses the company headquarters, client center, domestic branch network, overseas subsidiaries and representative offices, and other domestic and overseas business sites where Mirae Asset Securities performs business activities. However, as for global business sites, such as overseas subsidiaries and representative offices, the scope of reporting only includes business performance since data collection is difficult due to physical limitations in terms of material management and assessment.

## Independent Assurance

This report has been assured by BSI, an independent assurance service provider, to enhance its internal and external credibility. The Independent Assurance Statement is attached in the ESG Performance section (p. 126-128).

## CEO Message

<!-- image -->

## Dear valued clients, shareholders, and stakeholders of Mirae Asset Securities,

Since its establishment in 1999, Mirae Asset Securities has implemented management that considers clients, shareholders, employees, and society with the belief that innovation and investment can change the future. As a leader with the largest equity capital of KRW 10.6 trillion in the domestic securities industry, we are growing to become a global top-tier IB.

Amid the unstable conditions caused by the COVID-19 pandemic in 2021, based on immaculate risk management and differentiated business portfolio, Mirae Asset Securities achieved a record of exceeding KRW 1 trillion in income before tax for the first time in Korea's securities industry for two consecutive years. In addition, we have made remarkable achievements, such as customer assets of KRW 426 trillion, digital customer base growth, and 20% increase in income before tax of overseas subsidiaries compared to the previous year. As a result, we have strategically accelerated our global business expansion, expanded investments in global prime assets and innovative and growth companies, and strengthened digital transformation and pension businesses.

In 2021, we reorganized our 'ESG Policy Framework' with the aim of risk management and business opportunity creation. We set our 2021 targets at KRW 10 trillion for 'sustainable finance' created by each business, and raised these targets to KRW 45 trillion this year as we achieved it early. In order to respond to climate change issues, we joined the global RE100 renewable energy initiative for the first time in the industry, and developed a plan to procure 100% renewable energy (Scope 2) by 2025. To this end, we will actively help our stakeholders enhance flexible systems towards carbon neutrality.

Furthermore, we have strengthened education and services for the financially disadvantaged, and announced a shareholder return policy with the goal of 'returning 30% of adjusted net income' to increase shareholder value. Going forward, we will continue to practice warm capitalism to create a society where we grow together with our neighbors. We will also consistently implement a predictable shareholder return policy to repay our shareholders who continue to show their trust and support in the company.

In recognition of these non-financial performances, Mirae Asset Securities has been selected for inclusion in the Dow Jones Sustainability World Index (DJSI World) for ten consecutive years.

Peoples' happiness anticipating the end of COVID-19 was just momentary, and the world is now concerned about a deep recession caused by the Russia-Ukraine War and inflation. Keeping in mind that we have a responsibility and an obligation to create a better future for our clients, shareholders, and stakeholders, we will seek solutions through investment to ensure people coexist and prosper together in the midst of crises. We will do our best as a specialized investment company to improve your wealth and build a healthy society and environment by providing various ESG-based investment opportunities.

<!-- image -->

Chairman of Mirae Asset Securities Hyun Man Choi

## Investment Principles

Mirae Asset is striving to adhere to consistent investment principles and provide our clients with greater value.

Mirae Asset identifies the sustainable competitiveness of companies.

<!-- image -->

-   Companies with 'sustainable competitiveness' refer to those with a competitive advantage in market share, business model, corporate governance, and earnings growth. Share prices eventually converge with earnings growth and subsequent cash flow.
-   Thus, our effort to identify companies with sustainable competitiveness drives our search for high quality factors in a company that will lead to stable earnings growth.

Mirae Asset assesses investment risks with expected return.

<!-- image -->

-   Investment risks should always be simultaneously assessed with expected return. Key factors such as valuation, corporate governance and liquidity should be continuously monitored for any potential damage on the long-term competitiveness of a company.
-   Exposure to assets that may incur too much risk should be limited while the Risk Management Division should constantly monitor whether actual investment adheres to our investment principles.
-   A long-term perspective is needed when determining whether a company's competitiveness is sustainable. Although share prices may be volatile over the short term, earnings growth is the main driver behind long-term price movements.
-   Investing in an individual company's intrinsic competitiveness and generating stable cash flow is the very essence of long-term investment.
-   Successful investment management at Mirae Asset is not based on an individual's decision-making but rather on a team's decision to firmly adhere to our investment principles and processes.
-   Mirae Asset's investment is made with a portfolio in accordance with our investment principles through extensive discussions and teamwork among division members.

Mirae Asset invests with a long-term perspective.

<!-- image -->

Mirae Asset values a team-based approach in decision-making.

## WM

Mirae Asset Securities leads the wealth management market by carefully selecting financial products, diversifying investment portfolios and customizing consulting services.

## Business Overview

## Customized comprehensive financial consulting services

Our investment specialists at domestic branches and headquarter provide portfolios tailored to clients' investment propensities and purposes to ensure the successful asset management of our clients. Mirae Asset Securities provide investment solutions that respond to various types of investment needs of clients, such as domestic and foreign stocks, ETF, ELS and DLS, and prime alternative assets based on global networks, including competitive public offering funds carefully selected by the Product Selection Committee. In addition to safe and professional management services using labs, trusts, and OCIOs, we deliver comprehensive consulting services from real estate, legal, and tax experts on complicated issues such as gift, inheritance, and business succession.

## Top-level VIP Service

Mirae Asset Securities operates 'Mirae Asset Sage Club Membership', an exclusive service for VIP clients who pursue values beyond wealth and honor. The service provides deluxe leisure programs and optimal services such as private culture and art programs, and luxurious premium lifestyle services in addition to wealth management.

## No.1 Pension Manager

Mirae Asset Securities is the No. 1 retirement pension manager in the securities industry, offering the best pension solutions with Korea's largest pool of pension experts. We help corporate clients adopt pension schemes and support administrative issues such as accounting, labor, actuary, and taxation services, and earn a high rate of return through the provision of carefully selected products and continuous training for subscribers.

## Digital Asset Management Service

Mirae Asset Securities has established a comprehensive financial platform to enable clients to experience all aspects of investment in a digital environment. We create a customer-oriented mobile environment to make asset management, yield management, and advisory services more convenient in a digital environment, and provide AI-based hyper-personalization services. In addition, we are expanding financial services in a digital world in collaboration with heterogeneous industries.

<!-- image -->

## IB

We grow together with all our customers by providing the corporate clients with growth opportunity via financial solutions and by building a business model with which our investors can share the results of such growth.

## Business Overview

## Comprehensive consulting and financial services

We provide optimal financing solutions for each company and client needs based on our employee's expertise with ample industry experience. In addition to comprehensive consulting services such as M&amp;A, spin-offs, and corporate governance improvement, we offer various corporate financing solutions such as full range of services for initial public offerings (IPO), corporate bonds, issuance of equity linked notes (ETN), global bonds, rights offerings, real estate project financing (PF), and asset securitization.

## Various asset investment and financial products

We carry out alternative investments related to real assets such as aircraft, ships, power &amp; energy, and infrastructure, as well as Pre-IPO investments and mezzanine investments for domestic and global venture companies, based on our equity capital. We also provide financial products using various vehicles such as PEF (Private Equity Fund), SPC, and REITs in the capital market.

<!-- image -->

## Trading

As an investment expert, we provide financial products in response to clients' investment needs, and pursue stable and continuous profit expansion through linked management.

## Business Overview

## Financial product development and management

We provide clients with a variety of investment products including stocks, bonds, OTC derivatives, foreign currencies, credit, and carbon pricing based on our ample management experience. We are expanding our offerings to differentiated solutions at the level of advanced global financial institutions, such as issuance of derivative instruments using prime assets as underlying assets, and establishment of a Delta One trading organization. We strive to ensure that assets and profits grow independently through thorough risk management and improvement of management processes.

## Market creation and brokerage

We provide brokerage services for domestic and global stocks, bonds, and derivatives as well as ETF market creation based on our expertise and infrastructure. In order to create an investment portfolio tailored to our clients' risk preferences, we deliver optimal services such as credit extension, lending &amp; borrowing, currency exchange, and OTC derivatives for risk hedging.

<!-- image -->

## Global

Based on the accumulated know-hows deriving from our long-standing experience with global market and network, we are growing into a 'global IB' through establishing regionally localized business models and investing in prime assets such as those stemming from diverse and newly growing industries.

## Business Overview

## The most extensive global network

We have built a global network with 10 overseas subsidiaries and 3 representative offices in 10 countries across the U.S., Europe, South America, Asia, etc. As of the end of March 2022, there are around 1,400 professionals fully engaged in In diverse business areas such as global brokerage, IB, and Sales &amp; Trading.

## Business models with tailored localization

In order to flexibly respond to rapidly changing economic conditions and business ecosystems, we are building sustainable business models tailored to the circumstances of each region through in-depth localization based on our longstanding overseas business experience.

## Business for developed markets

In developed markets such as the U.S., United Kingdom, Hong Kong, and Singapore, we have been making continuous efforts to identify and invest in prime assets. In addition, we are taking the leap forward to become a global IB by expanding our IB business such as alternative investment and strengthening our Sales &amp; Trading business.

## Business in emerging markets

In emerging markets such as Indonesia, Vietnam, India, and Brazil, we are not only growing our customer base with advanced IT infrastructure based on brokerage services for local retail and institutional clients, but also expanding our WM, IB, and trading business. In addition, we are reinforcing our status as a full-service local securities firm by leveraging our localization strategy that takes local culture and economy into account.

<!-- image -->

## Principal Investment

We discover and invest in global innovative companies and prime assets based on the global network and experiences in corporate finance through IB deals at home and abroad, delivering stable profits to our clients.

## Business Overview

## Investment in new growth engine industries and innovative companies

We focus on principal investment by building an investment portfolio for global innovative companies with long-term growth potential. Through our global network, we are expanding investments in competitive prime assets in sectors with massive growth potential, such as the 4th industry, Web3.0, and ESG. We support industrial innovation through continuous investment in global assets and faithfully fulfill the role of financial capital.

## Global prime alternative investments

We are investing in competitive assets in a wide range of areas such as offices, hotels, logistics, and leisure by utilizing the group network. We pursue sustainable and stable profit creation with differentiated investment strategies from a longterm perspective.

## Digital

Mirae Asset Securities is leading the digital business innovation to offer clients with new investment experiences, taking 'digital transformation' as a company-wide core initiative.

<!-- image -->

## Digital Platform : Combine &amp; Redesign Experience

## Combined digital channels

In June 2022, Mirae Asset Securities will launch a platform by integrating three digital channels which have been operated for a different purpose: 'm.Stock' for domestic stocks, 'm.Global' for global stocks, and 'm.ALL' for financial products and pensions. We will operate the 'Mirae Asset Securities Integrated App' that manages all assets for retail clients. The App will be transforming into a platform where information on investment can be viewed on the home screen, from domestic to overseas, showing everything from financial products to all investment products.

## Data integration

The 'Mirae Asset Securities Integrated App' enables users to manage dispersed personal financial data at a glance through MyData, simplifying the search process so that product and investment information can be viewed in one place. This App recommends financial products that clients can invest in when searching for stocks, and provides investors with investment insights in the form of a feed using AI technology.

## Client experience renovation: investment service that starts from 'My'

The UX/UI of the App has been renovated to enable a 'customer usage flow' or 'experience'-centered journey away from a focus on functions. All menus have been completely reorganized around the user such as My information, My account, My product, Pre-Investment, Investing, and Post-Invest. We will continue to focus on the successful investment journey that starts with 'My' by developing various services that meet clients' needs.

## Data : Hyper-Customization Services

## Digital Solution Activities

## Launching and upgrading MyData service

Mirae Asset Securities was the first securities company in Korea to acquire the 'MyData' business license in January 2021 and launched the service in November of that year. The services such as the 'integrated asset inquiry' and 'My credit information inquiry' enable clients to engage with their wealth management in depth by congregating the dispersed financial data in one place. We will accelerate the development of competitive content based on the combined data.

## Establishing and upgrading Data Factory

In order to create a data-based innovative client management system and service, we have established a 'Data Factory' that collects, refines and processes vast amounts of client, product, and market data that did not exist before. It allows us to use data easily and conveniently in 'Tableau' or 'D-Portal' , and to provide better investment information and services to our clients through more sophisticated analysis.

## Building an AI engine

Mirae Asset Securities has built an AI engine that finds individual investment characteristics and interests to create 'super-personalization' services and content. In addition, we will deliver stable investment through rebalancing by adding robo-advisor and investment decision support functions. Going forward, we will continue to upgrade our AI engine to provide tailored information for each client and timely investment proposals.

## Mirae Asset Pay: 'Consumption is Investment'

Mirae Asset Securities opened the 'Mirae Asset Pay' service in October 2021. Mirae Asset Pay is Korea's first NFC simple payment service for iPhone that allows clients to make tag payments easily on their phone. Mirae Asset Securities will lower the boundaries of investment and broaden customer contact points through the 'Pay' service, making it part of our daily life. We are expanding our affiliation to include convenience stores, coffee shops and other partners that constitute general aspects of our everyday lives, while adding transportation card functions. Going beyond a simple payment service, we will provide a new customer experience that connects everyday consumption to investment.

## Innovative financial services, 'Global Stocks Mini'

We released a new financial service, 'Global Stocks Mini', in collaboration with fintech (QARA Soft) that is designated as an innovative financial service by the Financial Services Commission. Operating as a group buying option, the service allows users to apply for expensive foreign stock purchases during any time in a day without limits on the trading amounts, and enables fractional trading of global stocks with low transaction costs (transaction fees and currency exchange fees) by combining financial products and direct investment.

## Digital pension based on new content

Direct pension assets that were opened online grew 17 times in two years from KRW 106.9 billion in 2019 to KRW 1,861.8 billion at the end of 2021. We provide various digital content including YouTube to meet the growing client needs for online transactions. Under the slogan of Mirae Asset Securities, 'Pension to Invest', we are improving the customer experience by providing the 'Check Return on Pension Event' at all times. Content such as 'Raising your credit score by paying into your pension' is expected to greatly contribute to expanding the base of young pension clients.

## Company-wide Digital Transformation2.0

Mirae Asset Securities is strongly promoting company-wide Digital Transformation (DT) to lead the business in a rapidly changing digital world and to keep up with the global IB. We have prepared for DT promotion by declaring a digital vision, forming a dedicated organization, and establishing our talent, culture, and environment (DT 1.0). Starting from the second half of 2021, we entered DT 2.0, and we are promoting the integration of task solutions, the data-based decision making, the new way to work, the rapid response structure in the rapidly changing digital environment, and the integrated client system. After laying the foundation for digital transformation through DT 2.0, we will promote DT 3.0, which further expands on it at the HQ level.

<!-- image -->

## Client-centric Finance

## Client-centric Product Development

1)   MMF, special account and tax type funds excluded

Mirae Asset Securities is continuously innovating the financial consumer protection framework to practice 'Client First' , while striving to create a corporate-wide financial consumer protection culture, selling only competitive financial products that are selected based on strict standards. In addition, we continue to develop and provide new educational contents through various financial education channels to create a sound capital market by preventing financial accidents for the financially vulnerable.

## Product selection process

Mirae Asset Securities has put an excellent product selection process in place that is suited for long-term client needs and the global investment environment. We conduct the first scoring process by applying internal selection criteria on highly ranked funds that are appreciated in accordance with the results of quantitative evaluation on all available funds in the market. After going through a selection process based on the second scoring process, we receive final approval from the Product Selection Committee, and finally select a new product and recommended product line-up.

## Financial Selection Committee for Enacting Client First

The Product Selection Committee is a decision-making body that handles key issues of product strategy and selection of competitive public-placement funds to enhance clients' investment performance. It is composed of heads of the organization and performs related tasks such as product consulting, financial consumer protection, customer asset allocation, risk management, financial product sales strategy, and strategic planning for pension products, and is held at least once a quarter. A working-level consultative body under the Committee is held every month to analyze key issues related to client investment returns and establish the optimal product strategies. The working-level consultative body is led by more than 12 team leaders from product-related teams and risk management teams. In addition, WMs from sales branches also participate to communicate opinions at the field, enhancing the transparency of product strategies and practicing the value of Client First that the company aims to achieve.

## Selection of competitive public-placement funds

Mirae Asset Securities reviewed 1,130 1) public-placement funds on sale at the time of the declaration of Client First in June 2021 by applying an enhanced product selection process. As of the end of April 2022, the number of public-placement funds on sale decreased by 70% to about 320 funds, and we will only sell competitive financial products that meet the true value of clients.

## Enhancing Client Satisfaction

## Upgrading the CS system

Mirae Asset Securities has established our own customer satisfaction (CS) system for upholding the spirit of our Client First. We have prepared a system to further improve customer satisfaction, gradually and voluntarily, by conducting a satisfaction survey on clients who used our business processing and consulting services at our branches. This feedback was then reflected in the evaluation of branches. We operate programs that allow CS leaders at branches to improve customer expectations by sharing and giving feedback on the level of satisfaction through the CS system operation process. The CS pursued by Mirae Asset Securities starts with active efforts to ensure financial consumer protection. Ahead of the implementation of the Act on the Protection of Financial Consumers in 2021, all executives and employees made a pledge to practice 'zero incomplete sales' . As part of these efforts, we revised the customer satisfaction survey questions, by adding understanding on financial products and risks and satisfaction on the provision of financial product-related information, with the aim of 'Customer Satisfaction' .

<!-- image -->

## Consumer Protection System

## Improving Convenience for Clients

## Digital innovative branches with STM (Smart Teller Machine)

Mirae Asset Securities is preparing to lead in a digital environment where visiting customers can quickly process their major tasks on their own. As we introduced the first STM in the securities industry, it is expected that our clients will gain access to new opportunities.

## Client-friendly product instruction

In accordance with the Act on the Protection of Financial Consumers in 2021, Mirae Asset Securities newly introduced an 'Easy-to-understand financial product leaflet (summary)' by adding its unique icon that helps clients to intuitively recognize the key risks and elements of the financial products.

## Consumer protection culture

Mirae Asset Securities, which has been actively promoting a consumer protection culture since 2020, before the implementation of the Act on the Protection of Financial Consumers, continued its leadership in 2021. These efforts to protect consumers include the CEO's declaration to eradicate incomplete sales practices and employees' oath to practice consumer protection (February), video conference calls with heads of field organizations and sales branches (March-April), and branch events to promote the consumer protection practice (April). In addition, we held the Ceremony for Enactment of Client First (June) attended by the CEOs of major affiliates, led by Mirae Asset Securities. Putting these commitments into action immediately, we established a new Product Selection Committee (July) to carefully select competitive products, and took actions to prevent sales of products that were not selected.

## Financial ethics verification scheme

In order to establish financial ethics for employees, we introduced a financial ethics verification scheme and developed a curriculum for 4 months, and signed an MoU (Oct 2021) with a specialized agency (Korean Academy of Financial Consumers) to secure the credibility in the certification. We conducted the employee ethics training and certification test in January 2022, and 3,538 employees (99.8%) completed the training courses and 3,520 employees (99.6%) out of 3,535 employees passed the test. About 20,000 comments were posted over two weeks through various mobile-based content such as webtoon and card news and events were held to encourage excellent learners.

<!-- image -->

## Financial consumer protection governance

In order to practice our core value, the understanding that 'our existence is for our clients', Mirae Asset Securities organizes the financial consumer protection organization under the direct control of the CEO to secure the independence of business and build a company-wide collaboration system led by the CEO.

Since the establishment of internal control standards for financial consumer protection in September 2021, the 'Internal Control Committee for Financial Consumer Protection' was newly established and held biannually, and the results of the meeting were reported to the Board of Directors. In addition, we operate three authoritative bodies under Chief Consumer Officer (COO) to conduct specific discussions and deliberations on major issues - Internal Control Sub-Committee that has discussions on complying with internal control standards and management and supervision; Complex Financial Product Approval Committee that approves manufacturing and sales of complex financial products; and Financial Product Council that conducts review from the consumers' point of view before launching a new financial product.

<!-- image -->

## Innovations in financial consumer protection system

Mirae Asset Securities has developed a monitoring system to check the rate of return of newly launched and operated financial investment products, as well as the status of subscription by general and professional financial consumers. We are strengthening consumer protection activities by linking to the existing regular monitoring system and expanding monitoring personnel, appointing a Chief Consumer Officer as part of these efforts. We continue to innovate the financial consumer protection system to monitor the adequacy of the operation and sales process at an early stage, beyond the limitations of post-dispute monitoring.

## Client panel

In order to carry out our core value of 'Client First', we choose and operate client panels each year. In 2021, we changed the option to an online operation to respond to the COVID-19 environment. In doing so, we received various feedback on our products and services. We collected opinions on problems in online financial transactions and planned to improve financial services for elderly and disabled clients, and delivered key opinions to the relevant departments to reflect them in our business.

## Independent expert panel

Mirae Asset Securities has formed an independent expert panel consisting of three outside experts including professors to seek in-depth advice from academia on financial consumers and financial consumer protection. In 2021, we sought the opinion of an expert advisory group on measures to enact online financial consumer protection due to the proliferation of online transactions, and among them, we reflected various convenient services for elderly investors into our system.

## Improving Client Communication

## Spread of Financial Consumer Protection Culture

## Enhancing client complaints management

In order to protect the rights of financial consumers and handle complaints in a professional manner, we have set in place a dedicated team, the Financial Dispute Settlement Team, under the CCO (Chief Consumer Officer). The team serves as a control tower by identifying complaints captured through the complaints management system and internal communication channels and ensuring that response measures are taken in a timely manner. It also submits major issues to the Financial Consumer Protection Council to define measures and reports the results to the CEO and BOD. The team provides guidance through the 'complaint prevention guideline', which includes information on complaints and dispute cases, conducts mandatory annual training on complaints prevention, provides monthly updates on major complaints with case studies, and examines and analyzes data from complaints by types. In addition, the 'early complaint alert' system calls for enterprise-wide attention for issues that undermine consumer rights, as well as preventive measures to contain the spread of consumer rights breaches.

## Customer Service Improvement Council

Mirae Asset Securities customer management begins with practicing 'Clients First', which involves listening attentively to customer opinions on inconveniences and making improvements. Client opinions collected via a range of contact points and channels are passed to the related departments to be reviewed and improved and opinions that were not adopted by departments go through another round of deliberation by the Customer Service Improvement Council. In addition, we operate a client reward system for suggestions that have contributed to improving client service to improve the quality of client suggestions.

## Complaint prevention suggestion system

We operate a complaint prevention proposal system through which branch staff can identify various cases causing inconvenience to clients and unfair practices on the field and propose ideas to prevent complaints. Any suggestions can be submitted via the Intranet and those with effective suggestions and the most participation are given prizes (79 employees were awarded in 2021) to incentivize the workforce to avoid underestimating complaints and to strive for bringing about service improvement.

## Evaluation by the Financial Supervisory Service

Mirae Asset Securities is endeavoring to examine its consumer protection system and strengthen related functions based on the Assessment of Financial Consumer Protection Status conducted by the Financial Supervisory Service. After the enforcement of the Act on the Protection of Financial Consumers in March 2021, the Financial Supervisory Service introduced a self-assessment system in which the assessment is conducted every three years, but two of these years are self-assessed to submit the results. We were selected as a company subject to self-assessment on the actual state of financial consumer protection in 2021, and submitted a 'Good' rating for a total assessment of 127 items to the Financial Supervisory Service. In 2022, we will proactively respond to future evaluations on the actual state by upgrading all consumer protection matters in detail.

## Financial consumer protection evaluation

Mirae Asset Securities has diverse evaluation criteria to identify the status of financial consumer protection practices of HQ and branch employees in a quantitative and qualitative manner and is striving to improve the evaluation criteria each year in consideration of the practical opinions in the field and the company's policy directions. The evaluation is reflected in the KPIs of each organization and performs a powerful function that affects the performance and incentives of organization. In 2021, we secured fairness in the process of evaluation by employing separate evaluation methods for civil complaint-related departments and management, sales and operation departments, precluding potential disadvantages in the process.

## Pursuing Complete Selling

## Online educational contents for the financially disadvantaged

The outbreak of COVID-19 in 2020 posed significant challenges to our education programs for the underprivileged including the One Company, One School financial education program, which was one of our representative programs. Under the circumstances, in order to effectively convey the characteristics of financial products and investment risks in a non-face-to-face environment, we produced videos including 'Never do ELW' and 'ELS ETN, so easy even for Uncle Bob' and shared them with clients through YouTube. In addition, we will continue to develop and provide investment education content for the financial minorities such as senior citizens as well as product education videos.

## Training to prevent incomplete sales

Mirae Asset Securities recommends financial investment products tailored to financial consumers and provides various education and tasks to follow the sales process when selling. Branches conduct role-playing every month under the supervision of the branch manager to provide self-education to familiarize the complete sales process, and conduct mystery shopping for branch employees every quarter by an external evaluation agency. In case of branches with low results of the evaluation, we encourage them to send its own implementation plan to comply with the complete sales process and the results to prevent recurrence of the poor points. In addition, the Financial Consumer Protection Team provides standard sales process scripts for each product so that they can be used when selling financial products, providing VODs to provide financial education to consumers.

## On-site inspection and self-assessment

Mirae Asset Securities introduced a 'Chief Consumer Officer' to strengthen consumer protection, especially in the changing financial environment, and trained experts in assessment and education. The Chief Consumer Officer performs monitoring over the adequacy of the sales process, training to prevent incomplete sales, and improvements of the sales process. In case of failures or weaknesses in the sales process, the officer takes immediate corrective action, reflects it in the KPI evaluation according to the severity of the issue, provides training to prevent recurrence, and prevents incomplete sales. In addition, Mirae Asset Securities is computerizing and operating regular assessment indicators (indicators of concern about incomplete sales). The indicator consists of a total of eight indicators, including the ratio of high-risk products sold, the ratio of products sold to ineligible clients, the ratio of products sold to senior investors, and the ratio of complex products sold. In addition, the indicators provide various information such as client status, top sales stores, and top stores by period, and branches conduct monthly self-assessment to check the adequacy of sales process for each indicator.

## 2022 Financial Consumer Protection Activities and Strategies

## Establishing financial ethics verification scheme

Mirae Asset Securities is striving to establish the industry-first financial ethics verification scheme for employees as an annual training, rather than as a one-time training. The establishment of financial ethics is our first step in strengthening client trust, and we will make utmost efforts to offer better client-centric products and services based on this spirit.

## Financial education tailored to the needs of financial consumers

## Hi-Stock Class for high school students

Mirae Asset Securities is operating the Hi-Stock Class Program for high school students to enhance their understanding of the capital market and economy and to provide opportunities for them to grow into excellent future economic leaders. We produce standard lecture plans in cooperation with university professors and social studies teachers, as well as provide high school students with practical economics and financial education programs in and after class to ensure that they can grow into competent financial consumers. In order to increase the interest and participation rate of high school students, we are planning to conduct investment simulation with Mirae Asset MTS program and with an "up-down" simulation game as our education curriculum.

## 'Hi-Investment Challenge' for adolescents maturing social protection

Mirae Asset Securities will work with Social Solidarity Bank to provide financial education grounded on financial and simulative investment experience for adolescents maturing past social protection. Of the 50 participants selected for undertaking an online basic financial education course (pertaining to consumption habits, investment and credit management, financial fraud prevention), 10 students who have completed the course with distinction will be advancing with a higher-level course. In the advanced course, the participants will engage in theoretical education through actual investment, produce investment portfolio, and perform other activities such as work presentations while financial benefits totaling KRW 7 million will be provided as scholarship.

## CS system reorganization for practical customer satisfaction

Mirae Asset Securities is striving to prepare a system for listening to customers' opinions through various channels in line with the trend of increasing use of digital channels. We will conduct a client satisfaction survey that includes not only clients who have previously dealt with our business and consulted through branches, but also clients who have signed up for financial products through digital channels without facing employees. In addition, we will operate an efficient CS system that can substantially maximize customer satisfaction by reflecting the activities of CS leaders in the evaluation of each branch through a systematic evaluation process.

Mirae Asset Securities hires outstanding talent through diverse recruitment processes and provides ongoing training to enhance employees' financial expertise and digital competencies. In addition, we are striving to establish a healthy organizational culture that enables individual employees to manage their health, secure safety and health, and achieve work-life balance.

Since joining the UN Global Compact (UNGC) in June 2006, Mirae Asset Securities has fulfilled its social responsibilities by adhering to the UNGC's 10 principles in the areas of human rights, labor, the environment, and anti-corruption. Accordingly, Mirae Asset Securities established human rights principles consistent with global standards  1) and applies them across its operations to protect the human rights of all stakeholders, including employees, clients, shareholders, investors, transaction counterparties, partners, local communities, governments, and capital markets. Moreover, Mirae Asset Securities periodically reviews its compliance with human rights principles and has an internal process to ensure that any violations of human rights or discrimination issues that may arise are handled in accordance with established procedures.

## Human Rights Principles

-   Setting client success as a major standard of all business operations, Mirae Asset Securities continuously develops high-quality financial products and services that meet the demands and expectations of clients.
-     Mirae Asset Securities does not discriminate against clients on the basis of gender, religion, race, or political leaning and does not engage in immoral and unethical behaviors that might infringe on the rights of clients that it serves.
-     Mirae Asset Securities has administrative, technical, and physical measures in place to protect clients from the loss, theft, breach, alteration, or damage of personal information.
-   Mirae Asset Securities respects the free will and creativity of employees and does not discriminate against them on the basis of regional origin, family relations, academic background, gender, religion, age, disability, or marital status.
-   Mirae Asset Securities provides equal opportunities to employees according to their abilities and qualifications and implements fair performance evaluations to provide reasonable compensations.
-   Mirae Asset Securities creates a safe and pleasant workplace environment for employees and strives to provide a high-quality welfare system for work-life balance.
-   Mirae Asset Securities guarantees the freedom of association and the right to collective bargaining and does not treat employees unfavorably on the basis of union status, union activities, or the creation of a union.
-   Mirae Asset Securities implements reasonable decision-making processes and management activities based on a responsible, transparent, and ethical management system and does its best to maximize shareholder and investor value.
-   Mirae Asset Securities treats all shareholders and investors equitably and fairly to prevent undue violations of their interests and rights.
-   Mirae Asset Securities maintains mutual trust with shareholders and investors by disclosing management information transparently and accurately.
-   Mirae Asset Securities does not abuse its dominant position to make undue demands for the purpose of selling financial products and services, does not grant unfair favors to particular companies, and enters into and enforces contracts with integrity.
-   In its dealings with transaction counterparties and partners, Mirae Asset Securities conducts business fairly and in a spirit of mutual respect and win-win cooperation, thus helping to uphold the order of the market economy.
-   Mirae Asset Securities strictly protects and controls information obtained during the course of business with transaction counterparties and partners.
-   Based on respect for the culture and values of local communities, Mirae Asset Securities fulfills its social roles and responsibilities by undertaking CSR activities such as financial literacy programs for the socially marginalized, donations, and volunteer activities.
-   Mirae Asset Securities takes environmental protection into account as a key factor when establishing policies and procedures and contributes to environmental preservation by strictly complying with environmental regulations in Korea and abroad.
-   As part of its efforts to build a healthy society, Mirae Asset Securities strictly monitors and prohibits any exposure to situations
- that may lead to social problems such as child labor, forced labor, and human trafficking.
-   Mirae Asset Securities fulfills its basic responsibilities as a member of society, contributing to the development of the nation and capital markets through responsible management, job creation, and the faithful fulfillment of tax obligations.
-   Mirae Asset Securities contributes to the formation of a fair market economy by guarding against the use of undisclosed internal information for market manipulation, illegal money laundering, and bribery.
-   Mirae Asset Securities does not undermine its political neutrality by voicing support or campaigning for a particular political party or candidate.

## Human Rights Impact Assessment Process

Mirae Asset Securities identifies potential human rights issues across overall business management activities including business operation, value chain, and new business relations and takes corrective measures as necessary. We consider previous history of human rights violations, frequency of occurrence, and financial and non-financial severity and manage identified weaknesses by issue type. Related matters are rigorously managed by designated teams.

## Talent Acquisition

## Performance Evaluation

## Principles of fair recruitment

Mirae Asset Securities hires talent based on its principles of fair recruitment that do not place restrictions based on Academic credentials, gender, age. Throughout the recruitment stages, we proceed with coding test, competency based interviews and other systematic verification processes as required by the job descriptions to evaluate capacities of applicants in depth. In addition, we are continuously striving to secure excellent talent by conducting video interviews at all times in line with non-face-to-face work environment.

## Continuous overseas recruitment and strengthening IT / Digital recruitment

Mirae Asset Securities continuously recruit more overseas talent from global top -tier universities and expand acquiring more talent from abroad to secure diverse overseas investment opportunities. In addition, in order to lead the digital innovation for finance, we are expanding the recruitment of professional talent with IT/digital capabilities, including those with master's/doctoral degrees.

## Promoting social contribution activities

Mirae Asset Securities has provided students seeking jobs the opportunity to experience learning about the field by participating in the work experience program organized by the Ministry of Employment and Labor. In addition, through the internship program in the first and second half of the year, we provide professional financial education to students seeking jobs in the financial sector and continue to give opportunities to improve their job skills.

## Performance evaluation principle

Mirae Asset Securities pursues our mission statement to become a 'company that evaluates based on performance and compensates based on performance'. We are striving to fairly evaluate the achievements and capabilities of our employees based on transparency, fairness, and rationality, and to increase the evaluation acceptability. Employee evaluation is based on the achievement rate of individual management by objective (MBO), organizational contribution rate, and improvement in competency building. We have established a process to provide immediate and continuous feedback on the performance between the evaluator and the evaluates at least three times a year (2 interim evaluations, 1 final evaluation) to minimize bias that may appear in the evaluation process. In case the team head is deemed to have violated the process or the evaluation result is ill-grounded, the employee (evaluatee) may raise an objection to the HR team. In addition, we conduct a manager review for team heads and peer review and a 360-degree peer evaluation once a year.

## Fair promotion and compensation

Mirae Asset Securities reflects fair evaluation results in the salary adjustment rate and promotion score for the next year. We have improved the promotion system to adopt the 'selective promotion' under which employees are qualified for promotion only when they pass the performance criteria for certain period, replacing the past 'graduation-type promotion' where employees automatically become promotion candidates for the next job grade after a certain period of tenure. Such change has enabled us to establish a system where employees' continuous achievement becomes recognized irrespective of age, gender, and background, including competent young employees who are selected and promoted.

## Nurturing Talents

## Fostering-type Performance evaluation process

Mirae Asset Securities is striving to shorten the feedback cycle on employee growth and performance by upgrading the evaluation process, which includes setting goals twice a year and conducting evaluations three times a year, and carries out fostering-type evaluations in consideration of the CDP of employees, such as institutionalization of communication on employee strengths and supplements. It includes a goal-planning interview at the beginning of the year, interim evaluation interviews in July and October, and a final evaluation interview in December, and the team head encourages employees to benefit from coaching at all times.

## Selection of branch managers through in-house contest

Mirae Asset Securities selected branch managers through an in-house contest for the first time since its foundation, breaking away from the vertical competency evaluation and position-oriented HR management. It allows all employees to apply for the contest, and a total of 61 people, including those born in the 1990s, with different experiences, applied for the contest. New 15 branch managers were selected through the evaluation on branch management plan and online presentation screening, and 40% (6 persons) of female employees were selected. Through these efforts, we are contributing to gender equality in the workplace, and heading a shift in generation with excellent young talent with vision and expertise.

## Strengthening global competencies

## Language program for global talent development

Mirae Asset Securities operates an average of 352 language courses per month (English, Chinese, Japanese, Vietnamese, etc.), and 1,705 people per year took a total of 19,816 hours of education (404 courses).

## Program to sharpen global IB competitiveness

Mirae Asset Securities conducted two sessions of global IB basic course training in a year with the goal of becoming an IB that leads the global investment trend. In order to enhance our understanding of new growth areas and investment in various alternative assets, we conducted training on the topics of 'Understanding of Venture Capital Investment' and 'Real Estate, Infrastructure &amp; Alternative Investments' . Employees from different departments who wanted to learn IB business applied for it, and a total of 283 employees completed the 4,404-hour training.

## Strengthening financial expertise

## Global investment master course

This training course focuses on fostering our own competitive professionals through in-depth learning of global new growth industries. As the highest-level course consisting of top-notch lecturers in each field, only WM Employees selected through a strict screening process are eligible to attend. The course consists of approximately 40 hours of training, including learning about new growth industries, workshops, and team competition portfolio activities. Those who completed the course can provide customers with more reliable investment strategies and insights, and also can gain a strong competitive advantage. In 2021, a total of 129 students (three graduation classes) completed the course.

## WM Academy

Created to foster global investment experts, WM Academy is an intensive training program targeting all WM employees and was continued in 2021 with focus on global investment. Its content is developed to reflect timely topics and delivered through diverse lecturers, and in 2021, a total of 2,274 employees completed the course. WM Academy is the company's most renowned course representing WM training, and more than 2,000 employees have participated each year.

## Stock investment strategies of fund managers

This course is intended to improve basic investment capabilities and establish an investment philosophy that a WM employee must have thorough understanding of the changing industry and corporate cycle. This course was conducted under two themes of 'Key stock investing strategies' and 'Q, P, C practical industry and corporate analysis' . The training was delivered for a total of 9,660 hours and completed by 690 employees in 2021. We improved WM's overall investment capabilities by focusing on global industry-specific analysis.

## Seminar on promising industries

The seminar was conducted focusing on the market conditions to improve WM's stock investment capabilities and establish investment strategies through timely, promising industry analysis. The 'seminar on promising industries' was conducted via a real-time online video platform for WM branch managers and team leaders, focusing on content that helps employees in their practical work on up-to-date industries and stocks. In 2021, a total of 312 people participated in the online course.

## Professional financial data analysis course

## Digital Convergence for Financial Studies Course at Korea University

Since 2018, Mirae Asset Securities has been collaborating with Graduate School for Computer and Information Technology at Korea University to run a master's course on Digital Convergence for Financial Studies (outsourced course) with the purpose of internally developing the personnel who can spearhead the digital transformation of the firm. We enable employees to study several technologies such as digital technology management, data science, AI, block chain, and fintech, and present practical project design and implementation as graduation tasks.  By 2021, we have three graduation classes (56 graduates), and the fourth class (25 students) is currently studying in this program.

## Digital competency point system

Mirae Asset Securities implemented a digital competency point system for a systematic and quantified digital talent development. The digital competency points are given for internal and external DT education, participation in an in-house DT contest, work experience, qualification acquisition, and a master's degree or higher. We are striving to provide various benefits such as additional points for promotion, priority for selection of external advanced courses, and preferential treatment for transfers to internal departments according to the acquisition of digital competency points.

## Corporate culture of innovation and investment

## Innovation School

Innovation School is a course intended to encourage our employees to think outside the box and adopt new perspectives and viewpoints and thoroughly reconsider the need for global asset allocation. Its purpose is to promote a mindset of transformation and innovation for achieving Client First Alliance. This course applies to the entire Mirae Asset Group, and a total of 2,190 employees of Mirae Asset Securities have taken the course since 2018.

## A culture of communication in coexistence and harmony

## Building trust between employees and labor &amp; management

Mirae Asset Securities is aiming at continuous and sincere communication to create a horizontal culture of mutual respect. In addition, we operate regular Labor-Management council meetings to promote the welfare of employees and the sound development of the company. In 2021, more than 20 times of wages and welfare benefits negotiations were carried out. The union membership totaled 2,299 as of end of 2021, based on checkoff figures. In particular, we conducted a survey on 'positive organizational culture' to identify positive factors of the organization such as engagement and satisfaction and to build a corporate culture of innovation in 2021.

## Grievance handling program

Mirae Asset Securities operates a grievance handling program to listen to the voices of employees having difficulties and to solve their problems in practice. Any employee who has a grievance can feel free to report it, and the company has manuals and designated staffs in place for resolving grievances and protecting the employees. In addition, we have established and operated a grievance handling mechanism at all times to protect our client-facing employees. The company also conducts a mandatory all-staff training program for all employees at least once a year to prevent workplace bullying and sexual harassment, holding a special lecture for all employees in 2021.

## Family-friendly Corporate Culture

## Health &amp; Safety Culture

## Work-life balance

## Establishment of healthy work conditions

Mirae Asset Securities is implementing flexible working hours, such as flex-time work and lunch hours to create a flexible work environment where employees are empowered to manage their working hours and subsequently to increase work efficiency. We also encourage employees to take annual leave as they wish and provide regular leave, paid menstrual leave, and family care leave to help them achieve better work-life balance. We have implemented a work-from-home program, base offices, and a 'business casual' workplace to allow all employees to create a free and comfortable work environment,

## Maternity protection policies

Mirae Asset Securities has maternity protection policies in place to promote childbirth to address the social issue of low birth rates. To actively promote such policies, childbirth and parental leave exceeding the legal requirement are provided along with medical care costs for childbirth uncovered by government sponsorship. As of 2021, 138 employees were on parental leave, with 88% returning to work. Since 2011, we have been running workplace daycare facilities. Employees can also opt to work reduced hours for childcare, and this option has been encouraged since 2019. In addition, when employees need to take care of their own health, they can reduce their working hours, while health and examination leave for well-beings of pregnant women and female employees can be used as a paid leave.

## Improving welfare benefits

Mirae Asset Securities operates a sound benefit program which encompasses various allowances for congratulations and condolences, marriage vouchers, and wedding anniversary and other forms of aid such as housing support, medical expenses, and scholarships. Since 2021, we have been expanding the coverage of group insurance (cerebral hemorrhage, myocardial infarction) medical examinations and the scope of eligible employees (including employees under 35 added) to protect the health of all employees, providing personal pensions to help employees prepare for their retirement.

## Building a healthy workplace

## Employee health management and safety &amp; health system

Mirae Asset Securities establishes an annual safety and health plan and report it to the BOD, faithfully implementing the established plan and thoroughly complying with safety and health-related laws and regulations. In addition, we designate the CEO as the top manager and enable the dedicated team to handle overall affairs of the safety and health management system, disclosing policies for the health of employees and safe working environment.

## Mental health care programs

Mirae Asset Securities operates mental health care programs to address issues and psychological stress caused by work or daily life.  In 2021, 418 counseling programs were conducted for a total of 122 employees. The number of employees who received counseling increased by 84%, and the number of counseling increased by 104% compared to the previous year. In 2022, we plan to strengthen our mental health care program to reduce stress and improve mental health, especially for client-facing employees.

## Responses to COVID-19

Mirae Asset Securities has been trying its best to prevent its employees from COVID-19 infections. We have also implemented protective measures for pregnant employees and other employees susceptible to health risks through reduced work time and working-from-home systems, supporting the employees who tested positive to take necessary break by offering them paid leave. In 2021, we provided body temperature monitors and automatic hand sanitizers at all branches to actively prevent infectious diseases for our employees and clients.

## Sustainable Value Chain

As a specialized investment company, Mirae Asset Securities measures sustainable finance, which is recognized as directly and indirectly endorsing the achievement of the UN Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs). In 2021, Mirae Asset Securities set the goal of 'reaching KRW 10 trillion in sustainable finance by 2025' for the first time in the industry, and having achieved the goal in just a year, the target is further raised to KRW 45 trillion. Mirae Asset Securities is expanding the provision of corporate financial services and financial products that include ESG factors to major customers. We strive to contribute to the transition to a green economy and social value enhancement by laying the foundation for an innovative ESG financial market through investment and management.

## CLIMATE ENGAGEMENT

Since the Industrial Revolution, the average global temperature of the earth is rising due to the indiscriminate use of fossil fuels and overconsumption of goods by humans. This issue, called the 'climate crisis', has become a threat to human survival and has a growing impact on our lives. Mirae Asset Securities recognizes the increase in natural disasters due to climate change as a financial risk that may lead to a decline in asset values and damage to company values. In response to the climate crisis, we joined 'Global RE100' last year, and declared that we will convert 100% of our electricity consumption to renewable energy by 2025.

## Impact Investment &amp; Transition Finance

Mirae Asset Securities will faithfully play a leading role as a specialized investment company to achieve carbon neutrality (Net-Zero) by 2050.

## Signing a joint venture with TerraPower

Mirae Asset Securities established TerraPower, a joint venture corporation, in September 2021 to promote the acquisition, development, and joint operation of small and medium-sized solar power generation projects in Korea in collaboration with Bright Energy Partners (BEP). By doing this, we will integrate and lead the relevant market by combining the business capabilities that BEP preemptively built and our various financial solutions.

## Investing in Impossible Foods

Responding to climate change caused by meat consumption, the meat alternative market is emerging as a new consumption trend. The size of the global meat alternative market in 2021 was approximately USD 5.4 billion and  is expected to grow to approximately USD 23.4 billion by 2024 (source: Global Market Insights). In 2020, starting with attracting investment of USD 200 million in Impossible Foods, a meat alternative brand in the U.S., Mirae Asset Securities participated in the additional funding process and secured a 10% stake.

## Bookrunner of the largest corporations' ESG bond

Mirae Asset Securities is also actively raising funds for Korea's energy companies' transitions towards an eco-friendly system. Following last year, we engaged in the 'Green Bonds issued by Korea Western Power' as the sole bookrunner this year. In 2019, we participated as the underwriter of 'KEPCO ESG bonds', the first won-denominated among the Korea's energy companies, for 3 consecutive years (2019-2021). The funds raised by the issuance of the ESG bonds are being used for domestic and overseas new &amp; renewable energy business investments, facility expansion, and energy efficiency projects.

## Financial advisory and underwriting services on renewable energy and provision of eco-friendly financial products

With the establishment of the Adani's Rugby Run Solar Farm in Queensland, Australia in 2019, Mirae Asset Securities is providing optimal financial solutions to renewable energy market, such as providing financial advisory services for solar panels at the Hapcheon Dam in 2021. In addition, we provide eco-friendly financial products to retail clients. As of the end of 2021, we have provided funds worth KRW 236.5 billion and ETFs worth KRW 3,116.1 billion.

## Issuance of Sustainability Bonds

Mirae Asset Securities has become the first global securities company, to succeed in issuing dollar-denominated ESG bonds worth USD 300 million in 2019, while won-denominated social bonds worth of KRW 100 billion and global green bond worth USD 300 million were issued additionally in 2021. The raised funds are being invested in ecofriendly buildings, renewable energy projects, support for SMEs, and housing supply projects for the socially underprivileged.

## Joining carbon emissions trading market

Mirae Asset Securities joined the Korea Exchange as a trading brokerage member and started trading emission rights in December 2021 to stabilize the supply in the emission trading market. We strive to gradually contribute to reasonable carbon pricing in the market and to the supply of liquidity.

<!-- image -->

## Eco-friendly Management Campaign

In order to establish an ESG management system in 2021, Mirae Asset Securities established a new clause stating that 'environmental protection is considered as a key factor when establishing company guidelines' through the revision of the Code of Ethics. We are taking the lead in company-wide carbon reduction activities, and are conducting social contribution and environmental campaigns to encourage all employees to participate in climate change actions.

## Supporting global initiatives

Mirae Asset Securities analyzes environmental data such as GHG emissions, waste disposal, energy consumption, and investment asset portfolio management to set reduction targets and implement them in various ways. The targets are set in adherence to TCFD 1) and PCAF 2) guidelines. We aggregate and monitor risk exposure and carbon emissions by examining physical risks and implementation risks due to climate change. We are actively participating in efforts to help resolve the climate crisis by declaring TCFD and SBTi 3) in June 2021. Mirae Asset Securities has responded to CDP 4) for the first time in the securities industry in 2010, and by joining RE100 5) in 2021, we strive to contribute to the development of Korea's renewable energy market by participating in the Policy Working Group in collaboration with TCG (The Climate Group).

## GHG and Energy Management

Mirae Asset Securities has been focusing on energy consumption since 2008 and has disclosed environmental data in its sustainability report. Since the nature of finance &amp; investment industry involves relatively lower direct GHG emission (Scope 1) compared to other industries, indirect GHG emission (Scope 2) is instead at the core of the management. We are also voluntarily disclosing and managing the Scope 3 that covers business trip efficiency, paper and waste disposal reduction and other details. In particular, we are continuously upgrading the electronic document system, such as management information system (MIS) dashboard, to minimize the use of unnecessary documents. In addition, we strive to reduce energy consumption by conducting campaigns such as turning off lights at 7:00 p.m. and refraining from the individual use of air-conditioning units and heating devices.

## Water, Resources and Waste Management

Mirae Asset Securities manages residential water and household waste. Mirae Asset CENTER 1 Building has a system in place for using greywater and rainwater, and clean water that is used once and rainwater are purified to be reused for bathrooms and decorative purposes, thereby saving water. We have a sewage treatment facility inside the building that handles internal purification to minimize its negative impact on the environment. All the waste discharged is domestic waste, which is treated by an outsourced company, and wastewater is batch treated at a sewage treatment plant of the Seoul Metropolitan Government. We strive to minimize our environmental impact through thorough separation of garbage disposal and recycling.

## Employees' Environmental Awareness and Green Office Campaign

Mirae Asset Securities held an environmental slogan contest to raise employees' environmental awareness. As a result, we selected 'Our Small Actions, Painting our Green Future', and posted the 'Employees' Five Pledge' to encourage employees to practice environmentally friendly behaviors. At CENTER 1 Building where our headquarters is located, all fluorescent lamps were replaced by energy efficient LED lamps, and sensor-based dimmers were applied. In addition, we are saving energy consumption through ice thermal energy storage system using electricity at night. Starting with the adoption of five electric vehicles in 2021, we plan to convert our business vehicles to pollutionfree and eco-friendly vehicles. In addition, we are expanding our green office space, such as allocating parking spaces to provide car-sharing services and establishing ultra-high speed electronic vehicle charging stations. Going forward, we are planning to adopt eco-friendly materials inside and outside the building and acquire eco-friendly certifications in order to convert old buildings into a green building.

## Conservation of biodiversity and forests

Mirae Asset Securities recognizes the importance of conserving biodiversity and forest resources. We have set investment in illegal logging or logging activities as exclusion areas, supporting forest resource creation projects through employee participation, social contribution and donations. Last year, we donated to a project to transform a former landfill site, in front of Haeundae beach in Busan, into an arboretum. We also planted 190,000 trees of 634 species including zelkova to improve the environment and provide shelter to local residents.

1) TCFD : Task Force on Climate-related Financial Disclosure

2) PCAF : Partnership for Carbon Accounting Financials

3) SBTi : Science Based Targets initiative

5) RE100 : Renewable Electricity 100%

## Governance

## Governance System

## Board of Directors and Council

Mirae Asset Securities established the ESG Committee under the BOD on March 24, 2021. The ESG Committee establishes policies and strategies for Mirae Asset Securities' mid- to long-term vision for responding to climate change, and reviews and decides on climate change risks and opportunities across all areas of business.

The ESG Committee meets at least twice a year. In 2021, the Committee decided to establish the ESG Policy Framework that proposes the company's ESG management mission, mid-to long-term objectives and the Environmental &amp; Social Policy Statement (ESP) that defines the climate change risk management process. In the first half of 2022, the Committee reported on the revision of ESG Policy Framework and a RE100 Roadmap.

The ESG Executive Council deliberates and reviews overall ESG policies and strategies, including companywide policies related to climate change. The ESG Working Council executes strategic initiatives for responding to climate change and carries out various campaigns and trainings related to climate change to increase employee awareness and participation. The ESG Execution Team establishes the ESG and climate change-related policies and strategies, and sets environmental goals at all business sites and assigns roles and responsibilities to each sector. The Risk Management Division implements a review process that reflects climate change policies, and evaluates and monitors the impacts of climate risks in all business areas.

## Strategies

## The Role of CEO

The CEO of Mirae Asset Securities, the chairman of the ESG Committee and ESG Executive Council, pronounces mid- to long-term directions for eco-friendly management, establishes a company-wide environmental management system, and leads company-wide cooperation to quickly internalize the policies across every business unit.

## Performance evaluation and compensation

The annual performance evaluation and compensation of the CEO of Mirae Asset Securities is determined by the Remuneration Committee under the BOD, and reflects performance related to climate change responses. In the personnel evaluation of key positions, 'Sustainability Leadership' is evaluated, and ESG performance including climate change issues is reflected in the performance evaluation of the organization.

## Risk factors

Financial risk factors due to climate change are as follows. Physical risks involve economic cost and financial loss in real assets such as real estate and other investment assets. Transition risks that may occur in the course of shifting into low-carbon economy are impacts of related policies and regulations, changing perception in the market and stakeholders, and transition of investment assets into a low-carbon which may trigger severe impact on the company's financial value.

| Classification  Acute risks  Chronic risks  Scenario risks  Policy/scheme  Reputational risk  Technologies  Legal  Market (Portfolio)  Point  1)  Short-term  Mid-to long-term  -  Mid-to long-term  Short-term  Mid-to long-term  Mid-to long-term  Mid-to long-term  Risks  Physical risks  Transitional risks  Potential financial impact  Mirae Asset Securities classifies natural disasters including monsoon, heavy rains, and typhoons as acute  risks, and is aware of its negative impact on all stakeholders, and even our customers. In addition, operational  risks caused by floods, equipment failure, and building damage may result in direct financial loss, such as a  decrease in the value of holding office.  Mirae Asset Securities classifies financial loss due to temperature rise as chronic risks. This change may  increase electricity consumption across all industries, leading to a decline in operating profit with soaring  electricity prices, power shortages, and blackout accidents at the national level.  Mirae Asset Securities analyzed the potential impact and scale of climate change on the company in the mid-  and long-term with three scenarios of financial losses that may be caused by acute and chronic risks. If the  Earth	heals	itself	and	keeps	global	temperature	rise	below	2℃	by	actively	responding	to	climate	change	(RCP  2.6), natural disasters may occur in a relatively mild way It is estimated that the book value of holding office  would decrease by KRW 115.3 billion. If we substantially reduce GHG emissions (RCP 4.5), natural disasters  will occur and we are more likely to be exposed to climate-related risks. It is expected that the book value of  holding office will decrease by KRW 230.6 billion. Furthermore, if we do not make concerted efforts to cut GHG  emissions (RCP 8.5, BAU),  natural disasters will occur and we are highly exposed to climate-related risks. It is  also estimated that the book value of holding office will decrease by KRW 345.8 billion.  Additional costs may incur if the REC price rises while implementing RE100 to practice greenhouse gas  reduction. Given that the REC price as of May 2022 increases by 10% every year, it is expected that the cost of  KRW 300 to 500 million will occur every year (if 100% renewable energy procurement is achieved by 2025).  If stakeholders, including clients and investors, determine that Mirae Asset Securities' response to climate  change is insufficient, it may result in financial loss due to a decline in brand value and a decrease in demand  for goods and services.  In the process of transition to a more eco-friendly and low-carbon economy to achieve carbon neutrality,  additional costs for new technology development, R&amp;D, and investment in infrastructure facilities may incur.  Mirae Asset Securities may be financially affected by the level of low-carbon transition of investment target  companies.  If concerns about 'greenwashing' are raised by a climate campaign group, it may lead to litigation by clients or  investors. For instance, after the declaration of 'Coal Phase-out Finance' , if there is any loss due to insufficient  policy implementation or investment in stranded assets, legal costs may be incurred due to litigation by  shareholders and local civic groups and organizations.  Industries generating high carbon emissions such as steel manufacturing, cement, and coal power plant  may experience reduced enterprise value due to the rising carbon price in the future. Industries with high  GHG emission or enterprises with higher exposure to physical risks may incur financial losses due to value  deterioration in the future.   |
