## Risk Management

The Mirae Asset Securities' ESP excludes or restricts activities related to: environmental risks (environmental pollution such as air, land, and water, deforestation, biodiversity &amp; ecosystem loss, carbon emissions, global warming, and depletion of natural resources) and social issues (community destruction, child labor, forced labor &amp; human trafficking, vulnerability in occupational and community health and safety, and insecurity).

## Climate risk assessment &amp; comprehensive management process

The ESP principle is applied to all the investment, underwriting and advisory services of Mirae Asset Securities ('investment'). In addition to the industry, business, and financial analysis, comprehensive investment evaluation is made by identifying the purpose of financing, investment target, and business partner analysis according to the environment and social risk (ESR) classification criteria.

## [ESR classification criteria]

- 1 ESR [High]: High environmental and social risks. There is a possibility that it will have a negative impact that is difficult to recover in wide areas. There is a risk of undermining the company's reputation.
- 2 ESR [Medium]: Limited environmental and social risks. Negative impacts may be minimized through risk management, and recovery can easily be facilitated.
- 3 ESR [Low]: Few environmental and social risks.

In the case of 'high' or 'medium' ESR risk categories, specific impacts on related environmental and social risks are evaluated and review opinions are prepared. Review opinions require external expert's assurance on environmental and social impact, and in case it is necessary to mitigate and resolve environmental and social risks, supplementary matters may be reflected in investment conditions. After the investment conditions are confirmed, the department in charge continuously monitors the implementation of measures to reduce environmental and social risks.

Mirae Asset Securities is committed to reviewing risks and opportunities in accordance with the environmental and social risk (ESR) classification criteria, and fulfilling Socially Responsible Investments encompassing the communities that we live in. We have declared the 'Coal Phase-out Finance' to stop financing and investing in thermal coal power generation and infrastructure in March 2021. At the end of 2021, the coal exposure of our investment portfolio was identified as 0%.

Mirae Asset Securities reviewed a total of 200 projects in 2021 based on ESP , in accordance with the procedures of the sales department and relevant teams. Among them, 6 projects were classified as excluded or restricted activities under ESP (among ESR) and required additional review and transition plans. The dropped project was identified as a counterparty in areas where serious human rights violations have occurred or that mainly conducts coal mining business.

## ESP organization

- 1 The Investment Department confirms the ESR rating after discussing the ESR classification of the investment agenda with the Review Department (the ESG department, if required) prior to requesting the review.
- 2 When discussing, the Department considers ESP excluded or restricted activities, the degree of environmental and social risks, and undermining of the company's reputation.
- 3 In the case of 'high' or 'medium' ESR risk categories, the Investment Department evaluates specific impacts on environmental and social risks and submits review opinions.
- 4 If necessary, the Review Department may request additional data such as an environmental and social impact assessment report from an external expert.
- 5 The agenda may be submitted to the ESG Executive Council at the discretion of the CRO.

When the same coverage is applied, the exposures of the three industries: oil, steel and petrochemicals in FY2021 remain unchanged. It was confirmed that FY 2021 exposure increased due to enhanced coverage of high carbon sectors and high environmental impact.

The result of analysis on the exposure of high carbon sectors obtained by applying TCFD classification scheme to the asset portfolio, confirmed that the exposure was at 18.2% 1) .

## Scope 3: Financed Emission of Investment Portfolios

The transition to low carbon is not merely a matter of choice, but is a matter of survival. Countries around the world consider, the adoption of carbon tax or carbon border tax, and when the policy is implemented, the profitability of companies in the high carbon sectors may deteriorate significantly. Therefore, Mirae Asset Securities recognizes its role of specialized investment company, and measures the emissions of its investment assets as well as its own GHG emission management, and plans to achieve 2050 carbon neutrality through systematic management.

## Calculation of Financed Emission

Mirae Asset Securities adopts the asset classification system and climate risk calculation methodology defined by the PCAF to aggregate the financed emissions. As of the end of 2021, 38.8% of investment assets are classified as beneficiary certificates and are excluded from the calculation of financed emissions. For the remaining 61.2%, assets with access to GHG emission data accounted for 21.2%, on which we applied the PCAF data quality score, such as Score 1 (public data) &amp; Score 5 (average GHG emissions by industry based on national GHG emission statistics). As a result, the total financed emission for investment assets was 202,149 tonCO2eq. In the future, we will complete the SBTi verification by enhancing our 2050 carbonneutral strategy for carbon emissions in the asset portfolio.

## Financed emission data quality

Mirae Asset Securities calculates the data quality score of carbon emissions in its asset portfolio. It was confirmed that among the assets that correspond to the PCAF asset classification standards, the total coverage of assets with quantifiable carbon emission data accounted for 21.2%, and investment assets to which Score 1 was applied accounted for 89.5%, and investment assets to which Score 5 was applied accounted for 10.5%. The data quality score closer to zero indicates higher confidence, and the total score was 1.42 points, which is a reliable estimate of carbon emissions.

## INCLUSIVE GROWTH

Mirae Asset Securities is taking an inclusive approach to address social issues such as aging society, financial exclusion, employment crisis, and climate change, as well as enhancing the level of customized support for financial minorities. We are paying keen attention to providing support to small and medium-sized enterprises (SMEs) and venture companies hit by COVID-19 while rolling out CSR activities in our areas of expertise such as diversification of customized financial education content to improve financial knowledge for each generation. Going forward, we are carrying out ESG environmental campaigns in our daily life to create a sustainable future.

## Expanding Inclusive Finance

## Socially Responsible Investing (SRI) funds

Mirae Asset Securities is taking part in the nation's development of a better future by financing SMEs and venture companies in new growth industries, such as service platform, Deep Tech, ICT convergence, bio &amp; healthcare. In addition, by financing 'Mirae Asset New Growth Venture Investment Corp. Trust', we are actively supporting job creation, contributing to the creation of social value. We have made co-investments with Mirae Asset Venture Investment and Mirae Asset Capital since 2016 with the goal of providing clients with opportunities to make investments in SMEs or start-ups with growth potential while also opening up financing opportunities for them.

## Financial products for the super-aged society

Mirae Asset Securities offers 'Mirae Asset TDF (Target Date Fund) Series:, pension funds that are structured automatically to glide down to more conservative mix of investment from risky assets as the target retirement date nears. Unlike most of the TDF funds available in Korea, which are jointly managed with global wealth management company, and their products, Mirae Asset Strategic Allocation TDF Series are managed solely by dedicated teams, fully incorporating the perspectives of Korean investors.

<!-- image -->

Business Case

## MoU with KwangWoon Foundation (May 16, 2022)

Mirae Asset Securities has signed an MoU with KwangWoon Foundation to provide necessary financial education to all local community stakeholders to contribute to the establishment of better investment culture and stable wealth management. We provide differentiated financial education based on systematic wealth management method for parents and school staffs before and after retirement, imparting students with proper understanding of financial investment to ensure that they can grow into responsible economic leaders. In particular, we will conduct education programs based on a practical curriculum in our efforts to design the future as we transition into a digital economy.

<!-- image -->

## Improving the Accessibility of Financial Services

## Services customized for the financial minorities

In order to support the financial minorities (including the elderly and the disabled) who are relatively vulnerable to financial information, Mirae Asset Securities is making efforts to improve their right to know by providing educations and guidances; lectures on how to prevent and respond accordingly; guidances on types of financial fraud, such as voice phishing, smishing, and pharming; and manuals for different types of disabilities such as braille guide for the visually impaired, and ARS braille code chart. In addition, we have operated a consultation window exclusively for senior investors aged 70 and over since March 2022, and provide services that enable the counselor to respond immediately without going through the button-type ARS guidance.

## 'One Company, One School' financial education

Mirae Asset Securities has expanded the 'One Company, One School' financial education program in alliance with 392 elementary, middle, and high schools nationwide since 2015. We also carry out various financial education programs for 84,347 students as of the end of April 2022 in order to help young students grow into smart financial consumers. We currently operate various experience programs in connection with the Korea Exchange (KRX) and the Korea Council for Investor Education, as well as headquarter experience programs that include visiting the trading room, broadcasting room, and VIP PB center. In recognition of the contribution, we have been

<!-- image -->

selected as an Excellent Educational Donation Institution by the Ministry of Education and the Korea Foundation for the Advancement of Science and Creativity for two consecutive years. In 2022, we are striving to secure professional financial education instructors and to expand sustainable financial education by assigning 30 employees and 10 executives.

## Corporate ESG Environmental Campaign

## 'Our Small Actions, Painting our Green Future'

Mirae Asset Securities is carrying out a corporate ESG environment campaign at all times. We selected the environmental slogan 'Our small actions, painting our green future' to through an employee contest, and posted the 'Employee's Five Pledges to encourage employees to participate in climate action and reduce GHG emissions in daily lives. We also created tumblers and distributed to all employees, and held events such as 'my own tumbler introduction' and 'photo proof of using tumblers inside and outside the office' . In addition, we made a quiz to raise environmental awareness so that KRW 10,000 per participant is to be donated to environmental groups. The collected donations were delivered to the Green Future Foundation and used for planting support projects to improve air quality in the city. Mirae Asset Securities donated the Seokdae Waste Landfill in Busan to a nature restoration project for creating an eco-friendly arboretum, and agreed to contribute to the local community for carbon neutrality and provide a shelter for people who are tired of COVID-19.

## Stakeholder Cooperation for Digital Finance

## New AI technologies through industry-academia cooperation

Since 2017, Mirae Asset Securities has been executing projects aimed at analyzing financial data and developing algorithms through the industry-academic cooperation on big data that we have formed with Seoul National University. In 2021, we started researching the latest natural language analysis techniques on text data and using AI to improve our client service development and business. As the natural language analysis techniques are also applied to news and client data, we plan to provide news feed services that find the most important news in the current market situation and provide customized services for each client.

## Financial Big Data Festival

As part of its social contribution, Mirae Asset Securities has held an Algorithm Programming Contest for college students every year since 2017, and offered benefits such as an internship program and exemption from document screening. In 2021, Mirae Asset Financial Group, including Mirae Asset Global Investments and Mirae Asset Life Insurance, jointly held the contest in a wide range of themes, reaching a new all-time high with over 1,130 participants. By providing data of about 1.5 million clients for which de-identification measures have been completed, we provided participating students with an opportunity to directly analyze client interests and preferences.

## 2021 College online supporters 'Mi-Chin Supporters'

Mirae Asset Securities held the '2021 Mi-Chin Supporters' event for college students who are our future talent. For domestic college (graduate) students who are interested in finance and digital fields, we have supported activities to enable students to create various content using 'ZEPETO', one of the Metaverse platforms, for about 4 months. We got a glimpse of the creative ideas and new perspectives of the MZ generation, and contributed to nurturing future talent by providing an internship program at Mirae Asset Securities as well as a prize for excellent students.

Based on the business philosophy by which 'We value our people and embrace the future with an open mind', Mirae Asset has been supporting the dreams of Korean talents through global exchange scholarship programs and practicing a sincere culture of sharing with the underprivileged in the local community. Under the two main areas of 'nurturing talent' and 'supporting future generations', we are carrying out scholarship and education support programs, and spreading a 'culture of sharing' in our daily lives through employees' voluntary donations and volunteer programs..

## Corporate Social Responsibility

## Nurturing talent

Although Mirae Asset Securities suspended the selection of scholarship students from the fall semester of 2020 to the fall semester of 2021 due to the COVID-19 pandemic, we have resumed the selection for the spring semester of 2022 due to continued interest of students and available COVID 19 measures such as vaccination. We selected a total of 200 scholarship students by using an AI-based video interview to screen and select applicants from various viewpoints, and held an online award ceremony via Metaverse platform to ensure a safe Scholarship Awards Ceremony. In 2022, two scholarships are scheduled for the fall semester of 2022 and the spring semester of 2023, and we will preferentially select those who plan to study abroad for robotics, artificial intelligence, bio, and big data in order to nurture future talent in the innovative industry.

## Supporting future generations

Due to the prolonged COVID-19, Mirae Asset Securities has been planning and promoting new programs to minimize the gap in children's learning and cultural experiences, and to provide a safe educational environment to prevent COVID-19 infection.

## Youth cultural experience support project: 200 child welfare facilities (4,314 persons)

Mirae Asset Securities has provided cultural experience kits including online education and hands-on activities and expenses for in-depth learning activities to help children with many difficulties in external experience activities so that they can experience various cultures and arts.

## 'My Bookstore' support project: 60 child welfare facilities (901 persons)

In order to deliver the pleasure and benefits of reading books to children who spend a lot of time at home due to the prolonged COVID-19, Mirae Asset Securities has carried out the 'My Bookstore' project to create their own reading space.

## Youth Vision project: 37 child welfare facilities (337 persons)

Mirae Asset Securities has conducted online and remote maker education so that children living in the era of the 4th industrial revolution can find their potential and develop their thinking skills based on AI and innovative technologies.

## Special aid to vulnerable social group hit by forest fires in North Gyeongsang Province and Gangwon Province

In March 2022, large-scale forest fires occurred in Uljin, North Gyeongbuk Province and in Gangneung, Gangwon Province. These have seriously damaged forests and caused many residents to lose their livelihoods. In response, Mirae Asset Group and its employees promptly conducted a special fundraising campaign to comfort the weary hearts of the victims and to help them return to their former lives.

## Culture of sharing

## Hope Chain Program

The Hope Chain Program, which has been in operation since 2018, has focused on non-face-to-face volunteer activities with the prolonged COVID-19 pandemic.

Mirae Asset Securities planned hands-on volunteer activities that our employees can do with their family at home, produced two types of pop-up books, and made a Kalimba, a musical instrument, to deliver it to children in low-income households. We continue to practice our warm capitalism by adding CSR activities as a mandatory course into the introductory course for new hires every year.

## Product donation campaign

Mirae Asset Securities has expanded the product donation campaign, which has been continuing for 4 years, to all regions nationwide since 2021. As a result, a total of 11,773 items were donated, and the converted donation amount reached KRW 32,523,900. This has led to the reduction of 64,751 kg of GHG emissions and the creation of social value by providing one month's salary for about 22 people with disabilities. The product donation campaign will be continuously conducted to circulate resources for the environment and create jobs for the disabled.

## Live online job training in securities industry for job seekers

In order to solve the problem of youth unemployment, Mirae Asset Securities has provided job training in the securities industry. This was done in the form of a non-face-to-face live lecture for 4,155 people from 22 universities in Seoul who wish to find a job in the financial sector, along with start-ups specializing in

job training. This received positive feedback from job seekers. We conducted lectures in the form of talent donations by incumbent experts who are in charge of IB (ECM, DCM, M&amp;A) and WM, which are the most preferred jobs in securities firms by college students, and invited these experts to share practical advice on practical introductions and career entry methods.

## ESG environmental campaign through employee engagement

## Forest care projects

In order to contribute to fine dust reduction and carbon offsetting in urban areas lacking green space, Mirae Asset Securities has been caring for the 'Mirae Asset Securities' Forest' with an area of 650m2 of land in the Ichon Hangang Park with the participation of employees and their families. We will continue volunteering every quarter to create a sustainable urban forest, and strive to provide a healthy resting place for citizens.

## Plogging activities

The term 'plogging' refers to an act of picking up trash while jogging or running to make our lives more environmentally friendly. To protect the environment and promote communication between employees, Mirae Asset Securities encouraged 682 employees to participate in the environmental campaign 10 times in Euljiro and Yeouido, where its headquarters are located. In this campaign, after calculating the amount of donation by matching the number of participants, a total of KRW 34.1 million was donated to a forest restoration organization in the area affected by the wildfire in Uljin and Samcheok by KRW 50,000 per participant. We will continue to implement programs to take walks with colleagues during lunch breaks, pick up trash, and make donations.

## GOVERNANCE &amp; ACCOUNTABILITY

Under the ESG governance system established to manage and supervise ESG-related agenda at the BOD, Mirae Asset Securities increased capacity to implement ESG management in a responsible, transparent, and ethical manner.

Moreover, we are striving to build trust with all stakeholders by proactively disclosing our ESG-related information and progress.

## ESG Governance

In March 2021, Mirae Asset Securities established the ESG Committee under its BOD to deliberate on and decide ESG-related agenda. The governance comprises four authoritative bodies - ESG Committee, ESG Executive Council, ESG Working Council, and ESG Implementation Team - to ensure the systematic management of ESG strategies.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## ESG Risk Management

Mirae Asset Securities is the first securities company in Korea to establish the Environmental &amp; Social Policy (ESP) Statement in March 2021, which stipulates our process to identify non-financial risks such as environmental and social risks in advance and respond in a preemptive and timely manner. We define the scope of classification as either restricted or excluded from investment in accordance with International Finance Cooperation (IFC) standards and apply them to all investments and advisory services that Mirae Asset Securities conducts.

ESG risk Management Process

<!-- image -->

Excluded or Restricted Areas under ESG

## Environmental Risks

-    Environmental (air, land, water, etc.) pollution
- Carbon emissions and climate change
-   Preservation of biodiversity and ecosystem management
- Depletion of natural resources, etc.
- Deforestation, etc.

## Social Risks

-    Preservation, public health, safety, and security of local communities Safety &amp; Security
-   Protection of indigenous people and natives
- Exploitation of child labor
- Forced labor
- Poor working conditions
- Protection of cultural heritages

## Expanding ESG Bonds Issuance

## Promoting support of environmental and social projects

Mirae Asset Securities, as a financial investment company, aims to make investments that can bring about a more sustainable future for society and environment through investments and asset management. To this end, we actively executed Socially Responsible Investments. In April 2019, we issued our USD-denominated Sustainability Bond (USD 300 million), becoming the world's first securities company to issue the Sustainability Bond, and it has been invested in green buildings, support for SMEs, and affordable housing projects. Based on the achievement, we issued KRWdenominated Social Bond (KRW 100 billion) in February 2021, and became the first securities company in Korea to receive the SB1 rating, the highest grade, from Korea Investors Service (KIS) Rating, thereby further boosting credibility with investors. In addition, in June 2021, we issued global Green Bond (USD 3000 million) to promote investment in infrastructure and ecofriendly energy projects such as climate change and renewable energy.

## Enhancing Shareholder's Return Policy

Mirae Asset Securities established the shareholder's return policy for three years from 2021 to 2023 to maintain predictability and consistency of the shareholder's return policy. With the aim of increasing shareholder value and expanding shareholder return, we set

<!-- image -->

Shareholder Return

| Classification  Shareholder return  Cash dividend  Stock cancellation  Total  2019  1,821  -  1,821  2020  1,301  1,504  2,805  2021  1,881  1,740  3,622   |

(Unit: KRW 100 million)

* Shareholder return is the sum of dividends and stock cancellation, and the shareholder return ratio is based on the adjusted net income.

- **    Adjusted net income = net income attributable to controlling interests (consolidated basis) - profits not accompanied by real cash flow (large-scale unrealized gains, gains on bargain purchases)

the shareholder return, the sum of dividends and stock cancellation, to at least 30% of adjusted net income.

Thus, we carry out dividends, stock buybacks and cancellations every year. In 2021, we paid dividends of KRW 188.1 billion and canceled stocks worth KRW 174 billion, resulting in a total shareholder return of KRW 362.2 billion, or 31.3% of adjusted net income.

## Enhancing Transparency on ESG Management

In  early 2021, Mirae Asset Securities not only established ESG governance, it also added and reorganized the 'Sustainability Management' section on the website to transparently disclose the status of ESG management. This section covers the ESG policy framework, including the mid- and long-term strategic direction for ESG management and three key areas, and provides stakeholders with ESG-related policies in one place, including the human rights policy and the code of ethics pronouncement, that have been revealed through multiple channels.

## Corporate Governance

## Roles of Board of Directors

## Composition of Board of Directors

Mirae Asset Securities has secured the expertise and diversity of the BOD by electing directors with experience and knowledge in a wide range of fields. We also established a responsible management system to promote its long-term growth by ensuring the independence of independent directors and periodically evaluating the BOD. In addition, we have built a transparent and sound governance structure to protect the interests of stakeholders, including customers, through continuous improvement.

Mirae Asset Securities has a transparent and sound governance structure to promote the corporate long term growth and protect the value of stakeholders. The roles of the BOD are as follows according to the rules of the BOD.

## [Article 3 (Authorization) of Board of Directors Regulation]

- ①	 	 The	BOD	shall	resolve	those	matters	prescribed	in	relevant	statutes	or	Articles	of	Incorporation	or	delegated	by	the Shareholders' Meeting, basic policies for the management of the Company and important matters in relation to the execution of its business.
- ②	 The	BOD	shall	supervise	the	Directors'	execution	of	their	duties.

As of June 2022, the BOD consists of three executive directors and four independent directors. In order to strengthen the expertise and diversity of the BOD, it is composed of a female independent director and experts in a wide range of fields such as finance, accounting, economy, and electrical engineering. Furthermore, we secure the independence of the BOD by composing a majority of the BOD of independent directors and designating an independent director as chairman. In addition, in accordance with Article 6 of the Act on Corporate Governance of Financial Companies, the appointment of independent directors is restricted if they have served as an independent director for 6 years or more at the same financial company, or if the combined tenure of the independent director exceeds 9 years at the financial company or its affiliates.

## Appointment of Independent Directors

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

In constituting our BOD, Mirae Asset Securities takes diversity into consideration, such as race, gender, religion, birthplace, and nationality in accordance with Internal Rule of Governance. The BOD is composed of directors with experience and knowledge in a wide range of fields so that it is not too concentrated on a specific common background or occupation. As of April 2022, the BOD consists of 3 experts in the financial investment, 2 experts in financial accounting, 1 expert in finance/economics, and 1 expert in new growth engines.

Independent directors are appointed at the general shareholders' meeting, and the candidates are selected by the BOD (executive directors) and the Corporate Governance &amp; Nominating Committee (independent directors). For appointing an independent director, it should be checked whether the qualifications required by the relevant laws, the articles of incorporation, and the internal rules of governance are met. Candidate for executive director should be selected with expertise and leadership. Candidate for independent director should be selected as a person who is capable of supervising directors and the company's management from an independent position, with an understanding of the financial industry and has abundant experience in specialized fields such as finance, accounting, law, risk, and IT , and has no interest in the company or the largest shareholder.

## Operation of Board of Directors and Committees

<!-- image -->

Committees Composition

## Board of Directors Operation

In 2021, a total of 21 board meetings were held, and 107 resolutions and agendas were resolved, with an attendance rate of 100%. A convocation notice regarding a meeting of the BOD is issued to each director prior to the BOD meeting, and the agenda is sent to all directors to review in advance. For the agenda of the BOD, executives in charge of related tasks attend the BOD meeting in person to explain specific details. When an independent director or the chairman of the BOD decides that the relevant staff or outside person needs to attend the meeting, they attend to help directors understand the agenda by providing supplementary explanations or answering questions.

## Committee Composition and Operation

The Corporate Governance &amp; Nominating Committee, the Audit Committee, then Risk Management Committee, and the Compensation Committee are in place under the BOD in accordance with Article 16 Section 1 of the Act on Corporate Governance of Financial Companies. The Audit Committee and Compensation Committee hold a majority of its directors as independent directors to secure independence and transparency, and at least one member of the Audit Committee must be an accounting or finance expert. The Corporate Governance &amp; Nominating Committee and the Risk Management Committee also consist of more than two-thirds of independent directors. Furthermore, in early 2021, the ESG Committee was newly established under the BOD to strengthen our ESG management. And, one of the independent directors serves as Chair of the Committee under the BOD of Mirae Asset Securities.

## Audit Committee

The Audit Committee supervises the legitimacy and propriety of the management's actions to help the management make rational management decisions. Made up of three independent directors who are qualified in terms of independence, the Committee members have professional backgrounds in economics and business administration, financial supervisory and accounting, and finance. There are grounds to leverage advice and consultation from external experts, if necessary. To enhance independence and fairness, in particular, the Committee members are appointed and dismissed by an AGM, and they execute their duties with independence from the BOD or execution bodies. Resolutions made by the Audit Committee cannot be objected by the BOD.

## Independence and Diversity of Board of Directors

## Independence Guideline for Independent Directors

Mirae Asset Securities complies with the Commercial Act and Act on Corporate Governance of Financial Companies, and comprehensively evaluates the independence of candidates and current independent directors on the basis of Independence Guidelines for Independent Directors that meet the global best practices. An independent director is considered independent if he or she meets  all of the following independence requirements.

<!-- image -->

Independence Requirements for Independent Directors

1. The director must not have been employed by the Company in an executive capacity in the past five year.
2. The director and his or her immediate family member must not have received compensation from the Company or its parent or subsidiaries in excess of $60,000 per year, other than those permitted by SEC Rule 4200 Definitions.
3. The immediate family of the director must not be an executive of the Company or its parent or subsidiaries.
4. The director must not be an advisor or consultant of the Company.
5. The director must not be an executive or employee of a firm that has signed a major advisory agreement or a technology partnership agreement with the Company.
6. The director must not have been employed by the Company's outside auditor in the past three years.
7. The director must not have any related interest on the agenda determined by the BOD.

## Securing Board of Directors Diversity

In order to make strategic decisions based on a flexible and broad perspective as a global IB, Mirae Asset Securities obtains and maintains the diversity of the BOD by taking the following principles into consideration.

- Ⅰ.	 	 The	BOD	consists	of	directors	that	do	not	represent	the	interest	of	a	specific	group	without	any	biases	toward any such common background as academic background, age, nationality, gender, religion, and place of birth or job positions.
- Ⅱ.	 	 The	BOD	is	consisted	of	directors	who	are	equipped	with	sufficient	field	experience	or	expertise	in	such	related areas as economics, business administration, law, finance, consumer protection, information technology, and new growth areas that are required to fulfill their duties as independent directors or auditors of a global investment institution.
- Ⅲ.	 	 The	BOD	has	a	dedicated	organization	to	support	its	operations	through	which	training	for	independent	directors are conducted on an ad-hoc or regular basis to ensure the directors can maintain their expertise in the financial industry.
- Ⅳ.	 	 The	BOD	convenes	meetings	in	a	regular	or	ad-hoc	basis	to	ensure	the	diversity	and	expertise	of	the	BOD	can	be converged and complemented by free expression of opinions among the independent directors.
- Ⅴ.	 	 The	BOD	conducts	regular	evaluation	to	monitor	whether	independent	directors'	diverse	work	experiences, expertise, and professional skills are converged in a balanced manner.

## Preventing Conflict of Interest

## Evaluation and Remuneration of Board of Directors

According to Article 397 Chapter 2 of the Commercial Act, Mirae Asset Securities mandates prior approval from the BOD with votes from at least two-thirds (2/3) of the directors if any director wishes to use the company's business opportunities that may benefit the company now or in the future for the interest of themselves or third party. In addition, Article 398 of the Commercial Act stipulates that directors or major shareholders wishing to engage in transactions with the company shall disclose such intention to the BOD in advance and obtain its approval with votes from at least two-thirds (2/3) of the directors. Accordingly, the 4th, 10th, and 16th board meetings held deliberated on the fairness of transactions and unanimously voted on the 'Comprehensive Approval of the Transactions with Key Shareholders' and 'Comprehensive Approval on Mass Internal Transactions under the Monopoly Regulation and Fair Trade Act'.

Article 26 of the Monopoly Regulation and Fair Trade Act mandates prior approval from the BOD if affiliates or conglomerates banned from doing cross-investment among affiliates engage in such transactions as funds, securities, assets, products, or services (large-scale internal transactions) exceeding KRW 5 billion or 5% of the larger between total equity capital and paid-in capital with affiliated persons. We have therefore obtained approval from the 2nd, 3rd, 4th, 7th, 8th, 10th, 12th, 13th, 14th, 15th, 16th, 18th, 19th, and 21st board meetings held on large-scale internal transactions of affiliates in accordance with the Monopoly Regulation and Fair Trade Act.

Mirae Asset Securities conducts an evaluation on the operation of the BOD and its committees prior to AGM. The evaluation proceeds in a way that each director evaluates items such as compliance and operation, provision and disclosure of management information, and independence on a 5-point scale and shares a comprehensive evaluation opinion. The evaluation of independent directors is composed of 'participation' and 'competency'. The competency evaluation is conducted in two ways; self-evaluation and evaluation between independent directors. A survey is conducted to evaluate the duties, attendance, expertise, understanding, responsibilities, and fairness of each independent director, and collect opinions from the comprehensive evaluation. In 2021, all independent directors attended 100% of all board and committee meetings, and all independent directors received excellent ratings in competency evaluation.

Compensation of directors and auditors is paid through the approval of the Compensation Committee and the BOD within the limit approved at the AGM.

The remuneration is calculated based on duties, activities and performance, as well as the position in the financial industry and the size of the company. However, in the case of independent directors, we do not separately pay performance remuneration based on the performance evaluation to secure the independence and fairness of their roles.

<!-- image -->

## Evaluation and Compensation of Executives (including CEO)

1)   Average number of people for the year

2)   Directors whose terms of office has expired or resigned are included

The performance of executives is evaluated by combining the company-wide performance and the performance of the organization in charge. Mirae Asset Securities comprehensively evaluate quantitative performance such as profit and loss, ROE, balance in client assets, employees productivity, business growth, and changes in stock price, as well as qualitative performance such as business innovation, client management, digital transformation, risk management, and internal control. In addition to achieving goals, we consider performance relative to the market.

Performance remuneration is calculated based on the results of profit and loss and evaluation results, and is confirmed by the decision of the Compensation Committee and the BOD. We also operate a deferred compensation system to avoid focusing on short-term performance. Those in key positions above the vicepresident, and employees in charge of the diversified financial investment whose accumulated performance remuneration for the current fiscal year exceeds KRW 100 million are subject to deferred compensation. For those subject to deferred compensation, 50% of the performance remuneration is paid in the current year, and the remaining 50% is deferred at the rate of 15%, 15%, and 20% for 3 years in a way linked to the company's stock price. However, independent directors, audit committee members, compliance officers and risk management officers do not link performance remuneration to business performance and are not subject to deferred compensation to ensure their independence.

## Ethical Management

## Ethical Management System

In order to strengthen the ESG management in 2021, Mirae Asset Securities has newly added and revised provisions related to 1) Anti-money laundering and anti-unfair trade, 2) Anti-trust and anticompetitive practices, and 3) environmental protection and disaster prevention in the Code of Ethics. In addition, we made utmost efforts to establish an 'ethical compliance culture' by implementing an oath to practice 'ethical compliance' for all employees and providing continuous education.

## Internal Control Committee

Following the legislation of the Act on Corporate Governance of Financial Institution, Mirae Asset Securities convenes Internal Control Committee meetings on a semi-annual basis. Consisting of 9 members, including the CEO(chair), Compliance Officer, and heads of core business divisions, the Committee discusses internal control assessment results. The Committee also reviews to reflect the results on employee evaluation, plan to respond to internal control vulnerabilities, and enhance employees' awareness of ethics and compliance.

## Compliance Council

This internal council consists of 12 members, with the head of Compliance Department as Chair of the Council, and holds meetings on a quarterly basis to fulfill its role of collecting opinions on the direction of the corporate-wide compliance policies, providing advisory and reviews on matters related to improving regulations both inside and outside the company, and sharing compliance issues.

<!-- image -->

Policies and Regulations on Ethical Management

## Code of Ethics

<!-- image -->

We have overhauled the Code of Ethics in line with international standards to put forward the standard based on ethical values for all employees to fulfill our roles and responsibilities in society.

## Code of Conduct

<!-- image -->

We have enacted the Code of Conduct to present clear guidelines to practice ethical management, such as fair execution of job duties, prohibition on obtaining unfair benefits, and fostering a healthy corporate culture.

## Rules of Ethics on Sales Practices

<!-- image -->

We have established the Rules of Ethics on Sales Practices to stipulate our internal rules and regulations on such matters as fair and accurate work execution, prevention of financial incidents, establishment of a system to prevent conflicts of interest, building of a faithful information provisioning process, efficient handling of complaints, and protection of investor information.

## Internal Control System

* Decreases in on-site audits due to COVID-19	in	2020-2021

Mirae Asset Securities removes potential risk factors and prevents financial accidents by regularly and frequently checking the company-wide internal accounting management system, financial consumer protection system, financial investment product development and sales process, and information security system. In addition, the results of the internal control system assessment and improvement plans are reported to the BOD, which manages and supervises whether the company is properly implementing ethical and compliance management.

- 1)   FATF (Financial Action Task Force): The global money laundering and terrorist financing watchdog

## Customer Due Diligence (CDD) for the prevention of money laundering and terrorist financing

Mirae Asset Securities implements a Customer Due Diligence (CDD) that reflects the Act on Reporting and Using Specific Financial Transaction Information, the Regulations on Anti-money Laundering, and Prohibition of the Financing of Terrorism and Proliferation of Weapons of Mass Destruction in order to prevent money laundering, etc. of financial products or services provided by the company in financial transactions with customers. We also pay reasonable steps to verify the identity of customers, and to confirm the purpose of the transaction and the actual owner. In addition, we assign risk grades for each customer through various evaluation criteria, and carry out an Enhanced Due Diligence (EDD) that verify transaction purpose, source of funds, and occupation (industry) for high-risk customers.

## Watchlist filtering

Mirae Asset Securities set up an internal process to perform verification of customers by comparing them against watchlists before the completion of financial transactions, and rejects a trade with clients who are on the Watchlist. We also update data on a daily and weekly basis through the Dow Jones' commercial database at the international level to thoroughly check all customers and confirm whether they are on the Watchlist.

1.   The List of Persons Subject to Restrictions on Financial Transactions Announced by the Financial Services Commission in the Act on Prohibition against the Financing of Terrorism and Proliferation of Weapons of Mass Destruction
2. The United Nations Consolidated List of Terrorists
3.   The FATF's List of Non-cooperative Countries and Territories and the List of Countries that Do Not Comply with the FATF Recommendations on the FATF Statement
4. The OFAC's SDN (Specially Designated Nationals or Blocked Persons) List
5. List of Foreign Political Figures
6. The Sanction List Announced by the Country where a Foreign Subsidiary is Located

## Internal control for clients from high-risk countries

Mirae Asset Securities refuses to trade with clients from high-risk countries with international sanctions such as the FATF's 1) black list and gray list in accordance with Article 43 of the Regulations on Anti-Money Laundering and Prohibition of the Financing of Terrorism and Proliferation of Weapons of Mass Destruction. However, we may accept transactions after obtaining approval from the head of the division or higher only when the transactional risk is significantly low. We conduct more thorough verification of financial transactions with countries and customers with significant deficiencies in accordance with the FATF statement.

| Target  Target countries (As of Feb 2019)  FATF's black list  (Iran, North Korea)  FATF's gray list  (30 countries including South Sudan,  The Bahamas, and Yemen)  Response  Non-tradable  Conditional trade (It is only possible  when the risk of money laundering  and terrorist financing is significantly  low and there is approval from the  head of the division or higher.)  1. ## Data retention system on anti-money laundering and terrorist financing

Mirae Asset Securities preserves relevant data for 5 years from the end of financial transactions in accordance with Article 8 of the Regulations on Anti-Money Laundering and Prohibition of the Financing of Terrorism and Proliferation of Weapons of Mass Destruction, and operate the procedures for the management of documents and materials subject to preservation.

## Culture of Ethics and Compliance

- 1)   A system in which employees are forced to take leave for a certain period to prevent incidents or to detect incidents at an early stage
- 2)   Prior to the completion of the financial contract, rejection or management approval is required after reviewing money laundering risk by checking whether they are on the watchlist (persons who are prohibited from financial transactions, terrorists, etc.).

Mirae Asset Securities is committed to enhancing the value of employees' integrity and spreading a culture of ethics and compliance through continuous training and self-assessment.

## Employee education related to ban on money laundering and terrorist financing

## Compulsory education

Mirae Asset Securities conducts compulsory training at least once a year, including intranet-based, non-faceto-face, and group training, for all employees and executives(including BOD members, management, branch/ department heads, compliance officers). We provide education on the major content with regard to the Regulations on Anti-money Laundering and Prohibition of the Financing of Terrorism and Proliferation of Weapons of Mass Destruction, the types of suspicious transactions, and the latest money laundering cases. After completing the course, we provide opportunities to retake the course and the test for employees who do not meet the completion standards so that we can maintain a 100% course completion rate.

## Ethics and Compliance Reporting

## Financial incidents reporting

Mirae Asset Securities operates various communication channels, such as the voice of customer (VOC) system, Financial Dispute Settlement Team, and complaints section on our website, in order to ensure safe financial transactions for our customers. In addition, the Compliance Department operates an internal reporting system and various programs to prevent financial incidents and to contribute to the eradication of corruption. The internal reporting system has several measures in place such as protecting whistle-blowers, reducing their liability, and offering rewards, and is rolled out across the enterprise.

## Protecting and rewarding whistle-blowers

## Protecting whistle-blowers

-    Maintain whistle-blower's anonymity and keep the report details confidential
-    Whistle-blowers will not be positioned in the same department and branch with the offender

## Rewarding whistle-blowers

-  Offer rewards
-  Provide advantages for performance evaluation
-  Grant the self-reported reduction of fines

## Ethical Management for Partner Companies

Fulfillment of Tax Payment Obligations

## Ethics training for partner companies

Mirae Asset Securities provides ethics training to employees of its partner companies in order to bolster ethical practices within the supply chain. In particular, we include an agreement to have the partner company conduct personal information security training more than once a year in signing a consignment contract. In addition, the Information Security Team conducts monthly information protection training under the theme of compliance to enhance the security awareness of employees of partner companies who work on-site at our business sites.

## Support for developing partner companies' Code of Ethics

Mirae Asset Securities has announced the details of our Code of Ethics on our website to help partner companies better understand it. In addition, we provide our policies and standards on ethical management to partner companies for reference purpose, upon request.

## Strengthening compliance monitoring for business partners

In order to ensure fair transactions in the case of contracts for goods and services with an estimated purchase amount of over KRW 20 million, Mirae Asset Securities has established and complied with operating standards in which we receive the 'Implementation of a Memorandum of Integrity Agreement' from business partners.

Mirae Asset Securities complies with our tax payment obligations for domestic business sites as well as our overseas business sites based on criteria set forth by the respective local governments. When tax laws are unclear, we get clarification from external tax experts to satisfy legal standards. We disclose income tax-related information through an audit report that is made available on the Data Analysis, Retrieval, and Transfer (DART) system. Financial statement and footnote information on the audit report includes the criteria for calculating income tax expenses, deferred tax assets, and liabilities. Furthermore, we manage risks that may arise in relation to taxation, and prevent tax-related risks by fulfilling our tax payment obligations in a fair and transparent way.  For international transactions with overseas subsidiaries, we observe domestic tax laws as well as the OECD Transfer Pricing Guidelines, and execute tax payment obligations in compliance with local laws. We have overseas subsidiaries in the U.S., Brazil, Hong Kong, and other countries, and we transparently disclose the amount of taxes we pay in each region.

## Risk Management

## Risk Management System

In the midst of growing macro-economic volatility due to recent upward pressure on global inflation, the decline in the attractiveness of the domestic stock market due to the strong dollar increased the risk of capital outflow. And the possibility of a money-move from securities companies to banks and insurance companies increased with the interest rate hikes. In addition, new types of asset markets such as blockchain-based Non-Fungible Tokens (NFT) are expanding. In line with the trend, Mirae Asset Securities has strengthened its communication channels for high-risk overseas assets and actively monitored the global market to respond to new issues in a timely manner. Furthermore, we are seeking investment opportunities for business diversification by preemptively pioneering new markets related to NFT-related financial products and wealth management services.

## Risk management governance

Mirae Asset Securities has set the Risk Management Committee, which is the prime decision-making entity on the company's risks under the BOD. It consists of more than three directors of which a majority should be independent directors. The Committee members are appointed and dismissed by the BOD, with one independent director chairing the Committee. The Committee is in charge of formulating a risk management strategy in line with the company's management strategy and supervising whether risk management operations are properly carried out. Major matters deliberated and decided by the Committee include establishing a basic risk management direction and strategies and inspecting implemented matters; establishing, amending, and abolishing general regulations related to risk management; setting the company's risk-adjusted capital and minimum line of asset soundness; establishing the company-wide risk appetite and loss limits and approving excess limits; approving and monitoring stress tests; and handling other significant issues related to the company's risk management system.

<!-- image -->

-   Deal with material issues and items delegated by the Committee concerning the review of investments including environmental, social and governance issues, and report the outcome and the progress of major activities to the Committee
-   Establish, amend, and abolish detailed rules related to assessment and followup management, and review matters related to financial investments of a certain size and their follow-up management
-    Deal with working-level issues and items delegated by the Committee concerning risk management of proprietary and client assets, and report the outcome and progress of major activities to the Committee
-    Establish, amend, and abolish detailed rules related to risk management; assign risk profiles to divisions and business sectors that fall within the risk appetite approved by the Committee; set and change risk limits commissioned by the Committee and approve cases that exceed risk limits; approve and monitor the appropriateness of company-wide risk measurements; Check temporary changes to the risk management system and its status; develop guidelines for risk-adjusted evaluation on the performance results of departments in charge of transactions; and set the risk appetite for client asset management

| -   Establish, amend, and abolish detailed rules related to assessment and follow- up management, and review matters related to financial investments of a certain size and their follow-up management  -    Review assumptions used in valuation and validate market changes and models  -    Set standards for handling of and the procedure for approval of financial products  -    Set standards for sales of financial products delegated by the Risk Management Steering Committee, and make decisions on related affairs including approval and limitation of sales  ## Financial Instrument Subcommittee  ## Investment Assessment Subcommittee  Valuation Subcommittee   |

## Risk Management System

## Risk Management Framework

- 1)   The difference of current assets and current liabilities that mature for each period
- 2)   A series of activities for recognizing and evaluating, based on standards, operational risks that may occur during business operations
- 3)   Indicators of operational risks in a quantitative way for monitoring of trends

## Integrated risk management system

Mirae Asset Securities operates its risk management system to carry out the calculation of total capital at risk, risk management, risk adjusted performance measurement (RAPM) and stress test to establish an integrated risk management system that exceeds 'Minimum Requirements for Risk Management at Securities Firms' by the financial supervisory authorities.

## Integrated Crisis Management System

In response to increasing global market volatility, Mirae Asset Securities has established an integrated crisis management system for preemptive and timely response to crisis situations. We have classified crisis situations into three categories -attention, caution, and emergency -based on 'financial market stability indicators' , 'internal soundness indicators' , and 'incident response management indicators' to set detailed execution plans for each level. We have ensured regular and practical monitoring by diversifying monitoring of domestic and foreign markets and selecting specific risk indicators that reflect the characteristics of each team and product, and then reported the monitoring results to the management.  In addition, we have developed and are operating a business continuity plan (BCP) at the company level in order to prevent risks that are caused by IT system failures as well as risks arising from natural disasters, such as fire, flood, and earthquakes. We also have created an emergency manual to ensure continuity and stability of business operations by providing seamless business services in case of any other system failures.

Mirae Asset Securities has categorized risks to be managed into market risk, credit risk, liquidity risk, operational risk and environmental/social risk in order to ensure systematic management of risks we face. In addition, management system and standards are established that are appropriate for the characteristics of each risk to execute appropriate recognition, analysis, evaluation, and control of risks.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## Market risk

-   We use standardized methods set in the Financial Investment Industry Regulations to measure market risks.
- We calculate internal model Value at Risk (VaR) by adopting a SAS Market Risk Management.

## Credit risk

-   We set exposure limits by counterparty type or credit rating. We also maintain an appropriate level of capital adequacy by controlling credit VaR limits based on standard models.
-   We identify and closely manage counterparties to which we are highly exposed by regularly monitoring their credit risk.

## Liquidity risk

-   We use liquidity gaps 1) and ratios as key indicators to measure liquidity risks. Also, teams in charge of risk management and finance regularly monitor risks and report them to senior management.
-   In addition to general liquidity risk management, we formulate contingency plans to deal with any liquidity	crises	that	may	arise.	We	have	classified	crisis	situations	into	three	levels	-	attention,	caution, and	emergency	-	depending	on	the	condition	of	the	company's	liquidity	and	the	level	of	stability	in financial markets. Specific action plans are established for each level to prepare for crisis situations.

## Operational risk

-   We manage and control operational risks through risk &amp; control self-assessment (RCSA) 2) , key risk indicator (KRI) 3) management, loss data management, and calculation of operational VaR based on internal models.
-   Regular RCSAs are carried out to gauge the level of risks of concern that each department faces. If an indication of risk is found, then countermeasures are established to control it preemptively.

## Environmental/Social risk

-   We implement the ESP (Environmental and Social Policy Statement) principles to pre-identify nonfinancial risks related to our business and respond in a preemptive and timely manner.
-   We have established investment principles that reflect non-financial risk factors to implement responsible investment that considers stakeholders.

## Culture of Risk Management

## Potential Risks

1)   A cyber security issue that occurred in December 2021; a vulnerability was found in a free open source program that supports the recording of user footsteps (e.g., access time, movement route, and comments) in the Java program

## Evaluation on risk management

Mirae Asset Securities promotes efficient use of resources and stable investment by setting various regulatory ratios and investment-related performance requested by the Financial Supervisory Service as KPIs. The representative regulatory ratio managed and supervised by the Financial Supervisory Service, such as NCR (consolidated basis, above 500%), KRW and foreign currency liquidity ratio (3M, above 100%), and comprehensive foreign exchange position (lower than 50% of equity capital) are linked with the KPIs. In addition, we strive to minimize losses through management of investment-related indicators such as the investment losses (lower than 1%) and follow-up monitoring at all times.

Based on analysis on potential environmental and social risks that Mirae Asset Securities faces, plans and strategies are established that allow us to effectively respond to long-term risk factors that influence our overall business to manage risks.

| Potential risks  Business and  social behavior  Impacts  caused by risks  Risk  response  activities  Collapse of asset market bubble  ·   COVID-19 economic stimulus packages have  increased market liquidity, and investments  in risky assets such as stocks and virtual  currencies have surged.  ·   As the concentration of funds in the capital  market intensified, the value of risky assets  recorded an all-time high. The financial  industry failing to respond promptly caused fatal losses  to corporate management such as losing credibility,  customer churn, and loss of competitiveness.  ·   We acquired the Personal Information and  Information Management System (ISMS-P)  certification in November 2021 to further  improve our company-wide level of personal  information protection.  ·   We conducted regular information protection  training for all employees to spread a  culture of information protection. ## Information Security

## Information Security Strategy and System

Due to the continued presence of COVID-19, the use of non-face-to-face financial platforms has increased rapidly and the size of the data industry has expanded. As a result, a new business market that utilizes data by promoting the combination of various types of data without boundaries between financial and non-financial industries is gaining attention. In addition, as the uses of digital assets, such as guarantee of ownership and transactions (NFT) beyond payment, settlement, and investment, is diversifying, their influence is also rapidly expanding. With the advent of DeFi, a blockchain-based decentralized financial service, the importance of digital innovation in financial services is growing. In line with the trend, Mirae Asset Securities is actively supporting financial innovation through the digital transformation of finance based on a solid cybersecurity risk management system, bolstering its security system throughout the business to ensure the stability of the financial system.

## Information security strategy

In order to deliver digital financial services in a secure and convenient manner , Mirae Asset Securities has established systematic information protection strategies and is carrying out information protection activities based on three key major tasks.

<!-- image -->

2022 Information Security Activities and Strategies

-  Enhance strategies to respond to various security threats in digital innovation and financial services
-    Strengthen capabilities to prevent cyber-infringement from becoming more intelligent and advanced, and secure rapid recovery capabilities to reduce the spread of risks in the event of an attack
-    Ensure integrated management of company-wide security policies by building information security portals and increase employee accessibility to information security policy
-    Advance security anomaly detection and threat control level through security event analysis

## Information security system

## Information Security Committee

Mirae Asset Securities has set the Information Protection Committee in place with the goal of strengthening information protection. Chaired by the Chief Information Security Officer (CISO), the Committee is composed of the department heads in responsible for IT system operation, development, and compliance, and the Committee activities are reported to the BOD. A total of 6 committee meetings were convened in 2021. The CISO manages overall information protection tasks, such as presenting IT security goals and directions, establishing and operating an information security management system, and analyzing and improving information security vulnerabilities.

| Classification  Roles  Major tasks  Description  ·    Deliberate and resolve matters critical to information protection and report the Committee activities  to the BOD  ·    Establish strategies and plans to ensure security in financial transactions and protect financial consumers  ·   Review vulnerability analysis and evaluation results on online financial infrastructure and action  plans for improvement measures  ·   Deliberate and resolve disciplinary measures for those involved in IT security breaches and related  regulation violations   |
|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

## Bolstering information security infrastructure

The personal information of clients is more than a means to identify them. This information is a part of client assets, and companies have a responsibility to protect it. To strengthen the safety of client information protection, we obtained and have been maintaining ISO 27001 certification, which is an international information protection management system standard. We also acquired not only Information Security Management System (ISMS) certification, which is a domestic certification system, but also ISMS-P (Personal Information and Information Management System) certification in November 2021 to further improve our company-wide level of personal information protection.

## Strengthening planning and strategic operation on information protection

Mirae Asset Securities is carrying out continuous efforts in terms of information protection planning and strategy in a bid to establish a company-wide security governance and a security risk management system. We also have clearly defined the roles and responsibilities for information protection to create a virtuous cycle of protecting information, while rolling out diverse internal and external activities to solidify the information security system and spreading an information security culture. A number of preparatory activities have been enhanced, such as security reviews, mock hacking attempts, and vulnerability checks to minimize security risks, while R&amp;D activities are carried out in various areas for improved response to newly emerging technologies.

## Spreading the Culture of Information Protection

Mirae Asset Securities is carrying out diverse activities to spread the culture of information protection across the company such as information protection and prevention of data leakage.

Major Activities for Spreading Information Protection Culture

## Proactive security governance system

Set evaluation criteria for each of four items: security control, training participation, security compliance, and security awareness in order to create a company-wide autonomous security culture, as well as implement an information security evaluation system that evaluates each department and branch of the headquarter once a year and reflects it in KPIs

<!-- image -->

Help employees independently enhance their awareness of the importance of security by conducting 'Information Security Selfinspection Day' to have all employees and partner companies conduct a security inspection

<!-- image -->

## Sharing security issues

Build a system for quick review and sharing of the latest security issues through 24/7 internal control by an information protection-specialist company and external control through the Financial Security Institute

<!-- image -->

## Monitoring excessive access to personal information

Prevent data leakage and misuse/abuse in advance by conducting a monthly monitoring to find out whether any employees have excessively searched for client's personal information such as account information, balance, and transaction history, and engage in other activities aimed at restricting the use of personal information for other purposes except business

<!-- image -->

## Identification and reporting of security issues

Encourage employees who identify information security issues to report immediately to the Information Security Department to ensure that the information security officer can review the details and take action Revise process security policy according to the procedure, if necessary

<!-- image -->

## Ethics and compliance violations

In the event of a breach of security laws and regulations, take disciplinary and sanction measures such as personnel measures in accordance with the internal standards under the principle of zero tolerance

## Information protection training

Mirae Asset Securities conducts online and offline information protection training that is customized for participants that consist of the entire workforce, including IT staff and staff in charge of information protection, as well as employees of partner companies. We also offer optimized security training that is above the level required by supervisory agencies. By having IT developers with relatively high security risks attend offline training provided by 3rd party education agencies specializing in information protection, we are making continuous efforts to help our employees increase their awareness and understanding on information protection. In addition, we strive to further boost the competencies in responding to security threats emerging from new online financial transaction services that will arise in the Fourth Industrial Revolution and cyber incidents gradually becoming more intelligent and sophisticated. At the same time, efforts are put in place to enhance the control of security threat factors by analyzing security issues based on big data.

## Activities to protect stakeholder information

## Mirae Asset Securities

-    Operate prevention measures against DDOS attacks and intrusion detection systems and conduct biannual regular inspection to safeguard our internal IT system against intrusion and hacking attacks, and adopt a high level of data encryption to prevent data leakage
-    Strengthen countermeasures against external hacking and internal information leakage incidents by separating the entire work network of the IT center, headquarters, and branch offices from the external internet network
-    Establish a data loss prevention (DLP) system to prevent external breaches of any document containing personal information, such as resident registration numbers, phone number and email address, and encrypt documents automatically through digital rights management (DRM) in order to block external viewing of information in case of document leakage
-    Categorize trainees into general staff, IT staff, information protection managers, executives, and partner companies, and conduct information protection training that is appropriate for each group
-    Make an annual contract with an information protection specialist for security consulting, and improve the level of information protection on a regular basis
-    Complete the first follow-up evaluation to maintain the international standard (ISO 27001) for the information protection and management system and acquire the domestic standard (ISMS-P) as part of our continued endeavors for information security
-    ISO 27001 (international): Assessment on 114 control items in 14 areas such as information protection management and operation
-    ISMS-P (domestic):  Assessment on 102 detailed items in 21 areas such as establishing the foundation for management system, risk management, etc.
-    Operate an anti-APT system to block malicious attacks, such as ransomware, and to prevent subsequent internal information leakage
-    Implement more stringent authority management schemes on personal computers to control installation and implementation of unauthorized programs which may contain malicious code
-    Continuously monitor security incident status to perform preventive activities against abnormal symptoms and behavior
-    Analyze various events within the company to detect security risks imposed by employees before they arise, and operate an anomaly-based intrusion detection system to identify security anomalies based on risk level

## Clients

-    Detect and analyze client's login information in real time by establishing the fraud detection system (FDS) for e-financial transactions, as well as establish a system to contact clients immediately if they are deemed fraudulent, identify the situation, and register the incident through an integrated system through which the internal information security unit, Client Support Center, and other financial institutions are linked
-  Inform about and publicize precautions against online financial fraud to protect clients' financial assets

## Partner companies

-    Provide training on information protection for all employees as well as major partner company employees to enhance awareness of information protection
-    Set a guideline for outsourced companies entrusted with personal information, and take actions against companies failing to meet minimum standards for correction or replace them
-  Expand field check-ups for external resources to minimize risks associated with outsourcing

## Significant increases in assets and profits

Following the bullish Korean and global stock market and increasing stock trading amounts, we have achieved sharp growth in WM assets and profits. The total client assets in our WM business reached KRW 405 trillion, an increase of KRW 90 trillion year-on-year, while net operating income exceeded record high amount of KRW 1.3 million, reaffirming its dominant position as the No.1 securities company in Korea. We have created a stable WM revenue system by strengthening the fee-based revenue base such as wraps and pension assets.

## Enhancing overseas stocks and pension assets

Overseas stocks and pension assets were KRW 24 trillion and KRW 24 trillion, respectively, an increase of KRW 8.7 trillion and KRW 7.2 trillion compared to those in the previous year. Mirae Asset Securities has achieved qualitative growth in assets by diversifying our clients' domestic stock based portfolio with global assets, and by improving returns for clients through vitalizing the pension investments with ETFs and TDFs. In addition, we continue to improve our infrastructure to enhance convenience in investing for the clients. We also expanded the pre-market trading hours of overseas stocks , commenced after-hours trading, and launched free real-time quote services not only in the U.S. but also in China.

## Digital channel innovation

Owing to an increasing number of investment apps, Mirae Asset Securities is strengthening marketing and services for digital channels. We achieved 4.27 million account openings, an increase of YoY 145%, and achieved 2.42 million MAU, an increase of YoY 93%. In addition, we launched Mirae Asset PAY, which is Korea's first NFC simple payment service for iPhone, extending customer contact points to life finance.

As the logistics centers in the metropolitan area have grown in the form of fulfillment centers and mega-hub terminals, in addition to contributing to the revitalization of investment in the initial development project of the logistics center, we are striving to expand investment and business in public real estate, which is relatively flexible to changes in the real estate environment.

## TRADING
